FINAL SHAPE against pre-loop main: `src/main.rs` 475/71, `src/next.rs` 408/33, a new `tests/unsafe_pairings_are_refused_and_omitted.rs` 1754/0, `README.md` 23/2, `CHANGELOG.md` 2/0. THE INCREMENT IS EXEMPTED BY ITS OWN `accepted-at-escalation` WAIVER `workflow-enforcement-tier-w2`, record-backed against the escalation record scoped to this increment, which is the eleventh such waiver in this plan and the second on this step.

FOUR-ROUND SCORECARD FOR CALIBRATION, AND IT IS THE MOST LOPSIDED THIS TASK HAS RECORDED. 24 valid findings across four rounds (9, 5, 6, 4), severities high, high, medium, high, so THE CEILING NEVER FELL FOR MORE THAN ONE ROUND AT A TIME. ZERO findings were dismissed in the whole loop and only THREE were ruled invalid, all three on the increment boundary rather than on their facts. THE CAUSE OF THE LENGTH IS ONE FUNCTION: `resume_roots` was rewritten FOUR TIMES and EVERY REWRITE PRODUCED A FINDING IN THE NEXT ROUND, three of them that round's only product defect. Rounds 2 and 4 each closed a `high` THE PREVIOUS ROUND'S FIX HAD OPENED, and the round 4 triager proved the round 4 leak BYTE-IDENTICAL to the round 2 one, reached through a different branch of the same function. The other twenty findings were test-coverage gaps on already-correct behaviour and prose whose scope had drifted past its evidence.

THE ADVERSARIAL-CONSTRUCTION LENS FOUND THE ONLY PRODUCT DEFECT IN EVERY SINGLE ROUND, four for four, and no reading lens found one in any round. THAT IS NOW THE STRONGEST CALIBRATION RESULT THIS TASK HOLDS, and it generalises the lesson recorded at the end of the plan-review loop rather than merely repeating it: on a CODE artifact whose correctness property is a NEGATIVE, a lens told to BREAK the thing is not one lens among several, IT IS THE ONE THAT WORKS, and the reading lenses earn their place by covering claims and coverage rather than by finding mechanism defects.

THE THREE-SHAPE FAMILY OF COVERAGE FAILURES IS THIS LOOP'S MOST TRANSFERABLE FINDING, because each shape defeated the checklist written for the one before it. ROUND 2: a lens generalised past THE DIMENSIONS ITS FIXTURE VARIED (216 invocations, but the source's position relative to `docs/plans` was never varied). ROUND 3: a lens measured three moved cells correctly and then adjudicated them against THE WRONG CONTROL, comparing to a neighbouring configuration instead of the pre-fix binary, and so reported "nothing changed" about a change it had itself measured. ROUND 4: a lens varied the right dimension, used the right control, MEASURED THE DEFECT, STATED IT CORRECTLY IN PROSE, and then wrote "the behaviour itself is fine", because it asked "is the admitted artifact the anchor's own?" instead of "WHAT DID THE REMOVED ROOT PREVIOUSLY REFUSE?". THE COMPOSITE RULE FOR FUTURE BRIEFS: name the dimensions, name the control, AND name the question the verification must answer, because a correct measurement can still be adjudicated away.

THE ESCAPE VALVE CAUGHT SOMETHING IN EVERY SINGLE DISPATCH OF THIS LOOP, and TWICE IT CAUGHT AN ORCHESTRATOR ERROR the review loop could not have caught: the round 2 fix pass measured that a decided prescription's named code location would open a new leak and departed from it, and the closing fix pass caught the orchestrator mis-transcribing a triage disposition in a way that would have turned a true sentence false. RECORD THIS AS THE ARGUMENT FOR THE ESCAPE VALVE'S EXISTENCE: a writer that reports what it was unsure about is the only mechanism in this workflow that reviews the orchestrator.

THE CLOSING FIX PASS IS IN AND THE ORCHESTRATOR REPRODUCED ITS FOUR LOAD-BEARING PROPERTIES FIRST-HAND (2026-08-05, one commit `894163d`). AGAINST THE ROUND 4 TIP it is 395 added and 24 removed across four files, of which 349 added lines are the two owed tests; against main the increment stands at 2662 added and 106 removed. THE PRODUCT CHANGE IS ONE TOKEN at `src/main.rs:1589`, `try_exists().unwrap_or(true)` to `unwrap_or(false)`, with `resume_roots`'s deciding rule, `on_disk.is_empty()` fallback and mapping BYTE-IDENTICAL to the round 4 tip. 420 tests passing with 0 failures, clippy silent, validators green, `render --check` up to date, ASCII clean, `git diff main..HEAD -- docs/` EMPTY.

THE ORCHESTRATOR BUILT THE PRE-FIX BINARY AND REPRODUCED THE LEAK ITSELF, having first constructed the WRONG ARRANGEMENT and got a refusal from both binaries; the leak needs THE RESOLVED ARTIFACT TO LIE UNDER THE ERRING ANCHOR'S ROOT, which means the erring anchor must be the `--source`, since `resolve_metrics_path` anchors source-first. In the correct arrangement, an erring `--source` in beta (a trailing slash on a file that exists, `ENOTDIR`) beside a missing `--plan` in alpha, THE PRE-FIX BINARY PRINTS `metrics: 3 records` AND `BETA PRIVATE STATE.` while the post-fix binary refuses both halves naming alpha's root. RECORDING THE FALSE START because it is the substance of the round 4 lesson: the arrangement that looks like the defect is not the one that reproduces it, and a reviewer who built only the first arrangement would have concluded there was nothing there.

THE THREE PRESERVATION PROOFS ALSO RE-RUN BY THE ORCHESTRATOR: `G-EMPTYROOT` still refuses a lone missing `--source` with foreign explicit `--metrics` and `--ledger-fragment`; `R3A-1`'s C0 and C1 still AGREE, both reading alpha's own 2 records and alpha's own block, with C1 carrying its note; C2 still refuses. The implementer's own battery was wider (three binaries, thirteen `ADV-1` configurations agreeing 13 of 13, and a control against `main` that differs on nearly every row so the fixture demonstrably shows change).

ORCHESTRATOR DEFECT (15), MINE, CAUGHT BY THE IMPLEMENTER'S ESCAPE VALVE. The closing brief told the implementer to fix "a third false clause in `README.md:236` / `CHANGELOG.md:23`". THAT INSTRUCTION WAS WRONG: the round 4 triage says at its section 4.3 that those clauses BECOME TRUE once `R4A-1` is fixed and that "only the doc comment needs editing", and the orchestrator had mis-transcribed section 6.3, which measured them against the PRE-FIX binary via `R4A-2`. FOLLOWING THE BRIEF LITERALLY WOULD HAVE CORRECTED A TRUE SENTENCE INTO A FALSE ONE. The implementer checked the triage against the brief, reported the conflict, and ADDED a definitional sentence about the `Err` case instead, which is true post-fix and closes a bound the claims lens's clause-by-clause sweep never varied. THIS IS THE SECOND TIME IN THIS LOOP A WRITER'S ESCAPE VALVE CAUGHT AN ORCHESTRATOR ERROR, after the round 2 fix pass caught the falsified `canonical_project_root` placement. THE STANDING LESSON: when a brief transcribes a triage, it must cite the triage's SECTION and DISPOSITION, not paraphrase a measurement, because a measurement taken against the pre-fix build says nothing about the post-fix one.

THE FIX WIDENED THE RECORDED SINGLE-ANCHOR `..` RESIDUAL BY EXACTLY ONE CASE, AND `Q-55-residualbound` DECIDED: RECORD THE WIDENED BOUND AND MERGE, over fixing the widening too and over putting it to a fresh decision. THE MEASUREMENT, taken with a proper control: the `..` anchor ALONE is UNCHANGED before and after; beside an anchor that IS on disk it is UNCHANGED; beside AN ANCHOR THAT EXISTS BUT CANNOT BE STAT'D the residual NOW SURVIVES, because an erring anchor no longer suppresses the ghost anchor's root. THE NEW BOUND, replacing the old "with no other supplied anchor that EXISTS": A `..` TRAVERSING A MISSING DIRECTORY, ON AN ANCHOR THAT IS NOT ON DISK, WITH NO OTHER SUPPLIED ANCHOR THAT IS ON DISK. The direction is a FALSE REFUSAL, so it is fail-safe; no code was changed there, the prose stating the bound was corrected, and normalising `..` was declined because `resolve_for_containment` is the function all three resolutions depend on and there is no round left to review a touch of it. DO NOT RE-RAISE WITHOUT NEW EVIDENCE.

THE TWO OWED TESTS ARE IN AND THEY PIN THE CLASSIFICATION RATHER THAN THE SPELLING. Trigger is a TRAILING SLASH ON A FILE THAT EXISTS (`ENOTDIR`), chosen over `EACCES` on the triager's own correction that `EACCES` reproduces the misclassification always but the LEAK only when the derived root contains the artifact; the trailing slash needs no permission manipulation, behaves identically for any uid, and leaves nothing chmod'd if the test panics. BOTH ORIENTATIONS are covered, which is the point, since the round 4 regression lens measured this mechanism in the `--source` orientation only and concluded the leak looked benign. Each test asserts the `could not be checked` note on stderr SO IT CANNOT PASS VACUOUSLY if the anchor ever stops landing in the `Err` class, and each carries a one-character control. FLIPPING THE TOKEN BACK REDS EXACTLY THESE TWO TESTS AND NOTHING ELSE, which is the guard the line did not have before: the triager had proved BOTH POLARITIES left 418 green.

THE LOOP ESCALATED AND THE HUMAN CLOSED IT (2026-08-05, escalation record plus receipt `q_id:"Q-55-inc2escalation"`). CHOSEN: SEND IT BACK FOR THE ONE-TOKEN FIX, THEN MERGE WITH NO FURTHER REVIEW ROUND, over reverting the round 3 narrowing and accepting `R3A-1` instead, over accepting the `high` and merging as-is, and over resuming the loop for more rounds. THE COUNTERS RETIRE RATHER THAN RESET, which is the branch that follows from a SPECIFIC FIX THAT CLOSES THE LOOP as against a RESUMPTION of it; the reset option was put explicitly as its own choice so the retirement is on the record rather than assumed, the same discipline applied when the reset option was declined at round 3.

THE REASONING PRESENTED AND ACCEPTED. The fix was MEASURED BY THE TRIAGER RATHER THAN PROPOSED, with the whole battery re-run green after applying it, and it is MONOTONE, so the `G-EMPTYROOT` empty-vector mode it might have been feared to reopen is unreachable BY CONSTRUCTION rather than by test. It closes the `high` and the `medium` together and makes the README and CHANGELOG clauses TRUE AS THEY ALREADY STAND, so no prose is authored to match a behaviour change. ROUND 3'S REASON FOR AVOIDING ANOTHER TOUCH OF THIS FUNCTION WAS TO PROTECT A REACHABLE TWO-CLEAN-ROUND PATH, AND THAT PATH NO LONGER EXISTS, so the argument for leaving a known `high` in place has lost its object. THE PARTIAL REVERT WAS DECLINED because it reinstates the false refusal the human's own `Q-55-emptyroot` text declined to burden users with and makes round 3 buy nothing; ACCEPTING THE `high` WAS DECLINED against sidecar line 303, which says passing this increment's own tests is not sufficient evidence for it, in the increment whose entire purpose is closing that defect class; RESUMING was declined as the expensive answer to a question the triager had already measured an answer to.

TWO RED-THEN-GREEN TESTS ARE OWED WITH THE FIX AND ARE NOT OPTIONAL, because the triager PROVED BY MUTATION THAT THE SUITE CANNOT SEE THIS LINE IN EITHER POLARITY: both `unwrap_or(true)` and `unwrap_or(false)` leave 418 tests green. A one-token change to a line no test can observe is exactly the shape this project's `Q-66` rule exists for, and the two-anchor `Err` case is owed IN BOTH ORIENTATIONS, since the orientation is what hid the defect from the round 4 regression lens.

ROUND 4 RAN AND IS `new_valid` (2026-08-05, record 273): FOUR valid findings after dedup, ONE `high`, ONE `medium` and TWO `low`, ZERO INVALID, nothing dismissed and nothing downgraded out of the `high` band, so NO backstop is owed. Streak still 0 of 2. THE LOOP HAS NOW SPENT FOUR ROUNDS WITHOUT A CLEAN ONE AND CONVERGENCE BEFORE THE CAP IS ARITHMETICALLY IMPOSSIBLE, since a clean round 5 would reach a streak of 1 against the 2 a `risky` increment needs. THIS ESCALATES.

`R4A-1` IS A REGRESSION THE ROUND 3 FIX INTRODUCED, AND THE TRIAGER PROVED SOMETHING STRONGER THAN THE LENS CLAIMED. `resume_roots`'s `try_exists().unwrap_or(true)` classifies an anchor that is NOT THERE BUT CANNOT BE STAT'D as ON DISK, which makes `on_disk` non-empty, SUPPRESSES the `on_disk.is_empty()` fallback, and DROPS the other supplied anchor's root although NO supplied anchor exists. Another project's log is counted and its `## RESUME STATE` echoed verbatim across all five surfaces at exit 0 with BOTH JSON reason fields `null`. THE TRIAGER BUILT FOUR BINARIES (HEAD, HEAD~1, HEAD~2, main) AND DIFFED HEAD'S LEAKED STDOUT AGAINST HEAD~2'S, THE BINARY `G-EMPTYROOT` WAS FILED AGAINST, AND THEY ARE BYTE-IDENTICAL. So on this population `R4A-1` is not merely similar to the closed round 2 `high`, IT IS IT, reached through a different branch of the same function.

THE TRIAGER ALSO SHOWED THE LENS UNDER-SOLD ITS OWN FINDING, which is the opposite of the usual correction. The lens framed the trigger as "one typo plus one unusual spelling"; the triager reproduced THE FULL LEAK WITH NO UNUSUAL SPELLING ANYWHERE: a `--source` naming A PLAN NOT YET WRITTEN, which is the ordinary state `Q-55-emptyroot` was granted for, beside a `--plan` naming a plan that GENUINELY EXISTS under a directory the process cannot traverse. One correction against the lens in the other direction: `EACCES` reproduces the misclassification always but the LEAK only when the derived root contains the artifact.

THE ORCHESTRATOR'S OWN PROPOSED EXPLANATION FOR THE MISS WAS REFUTED, AND THE TRUE CAUSE IS WORSE. The brief asked whether the regression lens missed `R4A-1` because "absent-but-unstat-able" and "present-but-unreadable" are different cells of its dimension list. THEY ARE NOT: `resume_roots` sees only `try_exists() == Err`, and the triager reproduced `R4A-1` in full using that lens's OWN third value. THE LENS'S COVERAGE WAS FINE AND IT MEASURED `R4A-1`'S EXACT MECHANISM in its own `R4R-2` case pair, STATED IT CORRECTLY IN PROSE, AND THEN WROTE "THE BEHAVIOUR ITSELF IS FINE". It ran the `Err` cell only in the orientation where the erring anchor is the `--source`, so the task name matches the admitted project and the leak looks benign. ITS FAILURE IS ADJUDICATION, NOT COVERAGE: it asked "is the admitted artifact the anchor's own?" instead of "WHAT DID THE REMOVED ROOT PREVIOUSLY REFUSE?". THAT IS THE THIRD DISTINCT SHAPE OF THE SAME FAMILY IN THIS LOOP, after dimensions (round 2) and controls (round 3), and it is the one no checklist catches: A LENS CAN VARY THE RIGHT DIMENSION, USE THE RIGHT CONTROL, MEASURE THE DEFECT, AND STILL ADJUDICATE IT AWAY BY ASKING THE WRONG QUESTION OF WHAT IT MEASURED.

THE REMEDY IS ONE TOKEN AND THE TRIAGER MEASURED IT RATHER THAN PROPOSING IT: `src/main.rs:1569`, `unwrap_or(true)` to `unwrap_or(false)`. IT IS A FIFTH TOUCH, NOT A FIFTH REWRITE, since the deciding rule, the fallback and the mapping are untouched, and it is MONOTONE (`on_disk` can only SHRINK), so `G-EMPTYROOT`'s empty-vector mode is unreachable BY CONSTRUCTION rather than by test. Applied and measured across the whole battery: 418 tests still green, `R4A-1` closed, `R4A-2` closed, `R3A-1`'s closure preserved on all three controls, `G-EMPTYROOT` closed, `ADV-1` closed. THE STING: THE SUITE CANNOT SEE THIS LINE AT ALL, proven by mutation that BOTH POLARITIES leave 418 green, so two red-then-green tests are owed with the fix.

THE REGRESSION LENS'S LARGE POSITIVE RESULT SURVIVED THE SPOT-CHECK AND ITS METHOD IMPROVED. It verified all six round 3 closures and all prior fourteen with controls it DEMONSTRATED could show a change, which is the round 3 lesson applied; the triager confirmed `G-EMPTYROOT` row-for-row across three binaries and found `ADV-1`'s split signature in 0 of 13 configurations against a control leaking 13 of 13, STRONGER than the lens's own 6 of 13. Its 45-cell sweep attributed every one of 21 differing cells to a stated break or an accepted cost, and it established that NO TEST'S MEANING CHANGED across all four commits.

THE ROUND 4 CLAIM-ACCURACY REVIEWER WROTE ITS FINDINGS FILE DIRECTLY INTO MAIN INSTEAD OF ITS WORKTREE, the first isolation breach by a spawned agent in this loop. The damage was nil (an untracked, ASCII-clean findings file, exactly the content the orchestrator would have merged, with no tracked file on main touched) and it was caught by the orchestrator noticing the path in the agent's own report. RECORDED BECAUSE THE RULE'S VALUE IS NOT MEASURED BY THIS INSTANCE'S HARM: main is supposed to stay pristine until the orchestrator integrates, and a spawned agent bypassing that is exactly what the isolation tier exists to prevent. The lesson for future briefs is that naming the findings path in ABSOLUTE form inside the agent's own worktree, rather than as a repository-relative path, would have removed the ambiguity.

THE ROUND 3 FIX PASS IS IN AND THE ORCHESTRATOR VERIFIED ITS TWO LOAD-BEARING PROPERTIES FIRST-HAND (2026-08-05, one commit `9c294a3`). AGAINST THE ROUND 3 TIP it is 274 added and 49 removed across four files; against main the increment stands at 2291 added and 106 removed, with 1405 of those the test file. THE PRODUCT LOGIC CHANGED IS TEN LINES ACROSS TWO FUNCTIONS and everything else is doc comments, two documentation clauses and tests. THE NARROWING ITSELF is six lines replacing five in `resume_roots`: collect the supplied anchors, filter to those on disk, USE THE ON-DISK SET WHEN IT IS NON-EMPTY AND THE FULL SUPPLIED SET OTHERWISE, then map as before. 418 tests passing with 0 failures, clippy silent, validators green, `render --check` up to date, ASCII clean, `git diff main..HEAD -- docs/` still EMPTY.

THE TRAP THE ORCHESTRATOR NAMED IN THE BRIEF WAS THE REAL RISK AND IT WAS HANDLED: the narrowing must not reopen `Q-55-emptyroot`, the round 2 `high`, which closed the leak where NO anchor canonicalises. VERIFIED BY THE ORCHESTRATOR ON ITS OWN FRESH FIXTURE rather than relayed: a LONE MISSING `--source` with a foreign `--metrics` and a foreign `--ledger-fragment` REFUSES BOTH, prints the note, and shows NO BETA CONTENT on any surface; and the round 3 defect is gone, with C0 (no `--plan`) and C1 (`--plan` supplied but missing) now BYTE-IDENTICAL, both reading alpha's own 2 records and alpha's own resume state, C1 additionally carrying the note. THE IMPLEMENTER PROVED THE SAME THING STRUCTURALLY WITH A MUTATION THAT NO PREVIOUS PASS THOUGHT TO RUN: over-applying the narrowing so only on-disk anchors ever decide REDS `an_anchor_that_does_not_exist_still_supplies_a_root` while leaving the new test green, and reverting the narrowing REDS the new test while leaving the old one green, SO THE TWO TESTS PULL IN OPPOSITE DIRECTIONS AND EACH CATCHES ITS OWN MUTATION. Neither closure rests on the other, which is the strongest form this evidence can take.

A NEW RESIDUAL IS MEASURED AND MUST BE RECORDED RATHER THAN ASSUMED CLOSED. The orchestrator asked whether the `R3A-1` narrowing incidentally closes `R3A-3`'s behavioural half, since the triager found they share one cause, and required a measurement either way. THE ANSWER IS PARTIAL, and the implementer varied the right dimension (whether a second EXISTING anchor accompanies the `ghost/..` anchor) with the right control (the same file spelled without `ghost/..`). CASE B, a ghost anchor BESIDE an existing one, is INCIDENTALLY CLOSED, but that case is `R3A-1`'s population rather than `R3A-3`'s. CASE A, THE GHOST ANCHOR ALONE, IS BYTE-IDENTICAL BEFORE AND AFTER, because with no other supplied anchor on disk the fallback still supplies the ghost root. SO THE RESIDUAL IS THE SINGLE-ANCHOR SPELLING: a `..` traversing a directory that does not exist, on an anchor that does not exist, with no other supplied anchor that does; the derived root is then a string that is not a directory and the project's own log is refused. It is written into `resolve_for_containment`'s doc comment in the direction it was measured, and `R3A-3`'S BEHAVIOURAL HALF STAYS DELIBERATELY UNFIXED on the triager's reasoning that normalising `..` touches the function all three resolutions depend on, which is not a round-3 change. DO NOT RE-RAISE IT WITHOUT NEW EVIDENCE.

FOUR THINGS THE FIX PASS RAISED AGAINST ITSELF, the escape valve having now produced something in every dispatch of this loop. (1) IT USED `try_exists().unwrap_or(true)` IN `resume_roots`, A DECISION THE TRIAGE DID NOT PRESCRIBE, on the reasoning that with the note now distinguishing three cases a bare `.exists()` in `resume_roots` would SILENTLY DROP an unreadable-metadata anchor's root while the note declined to call it missing, which is exactly the two-function gap a later round files; it chose "cannot tell" means present because of the two directions only that one ADDS a root rather than removing one. (2) IT TOOK THREE DOC SITES NO FINDING NAMED, because the narrowing made each false in a NEW way, so leaving them would have shipped a claim its own change falsified; these are consequences of `R3A-1` rather than separate improvements. (3) The new `chmod 000` test carries a MEASURED `opaque` GUARD so a root-user run does not assert against a fixture that did not take, at the cost of the assertion silently not running there. (4) `toml_source` STILL USES `Path::exists`, the third `exists()` call in this family, left alone because `R3A-2` was filed against `note_missing_anchors` and both branches reach the same fallback anyway; recorded because a reviewer may ask.

THE FIX PASS CHECKED RATHER THAN ASSUMED THAT NO SPECIFICATION CHANGE IS OWED, and the check is worth keeping: sidecar line 182 already scopes the pairing rule to "a `--source` and a `--plan` that BOTH EXIST must resolve to the SAME root, and with one alone the anchor is the root", which is what the narrowed code now does AND WHAT THE ROUND 3 TIP DID NOT. THE NARROWING MOVES THE CODE TOWARD THE SPECIFICATION TEXT RATHER THAN AWAY FROM IT, so nothing there needs rewording and the accepted-cost list stays at four.

ROUND 3'S ONE ROUTED DECISION IS TAKEN (2026-08-05, receipt `q_id:"Q-55-anchorveto"`): NARROW `resume_roots`, over accepting the false refusal as a fifth cost and over fixing it while RESETTING the round counter. THE FIX, about four lines at `src/main.rs:1509-1518`: a NONEXISTENT anchor contributes a root ONLY WHEN NO SUPPLIED ANCHOR EXISTS, which closes the false refusal while keeping the empty-vector case closed. THE REASONING PRESENTED: it restores the behaviour the human's own `Q-55-emptyroot` text said it wanted, since that decision declined the unpairable option expressly to avoid omitting an artifact legitimately belonging to the anchor's own directory; it is the narrowest correction available; and it removes a FAIL-SAFE-BUT-WRONG refusal on a project's OWN artifacts (Safe on existing projects, One source of truth, Minimal by default). THE COUNTERWEIGHT WAS STATED PLAINLY AND ACCEPTED RATHER THAN GLOSSED: `resume_roots` HAS NOW BEEN REWRITTEN TWICE AND EACH REWRITE PRODUCED A FINDING IN THE NEXT ROUND, so this is the ONLY item on round 3's list that could cost round 4 its cleanliness, and at streak 0 of 2 with two rounds left before the cap that risk is concrete. THE COUNTER-RESET OPTION WAS DECLINED DELIBERATELY, which matters because this project has recorded that picking reset by accident silently raises the bar; it was put as its own option so the choice not to reset is on the record.

THE CONVERGENCE ARITHMETIC FROM HERE, recorded so a resuming agent does not have to derive it: streak 0 of the 2 this `risky` increment needs, rounds 1 to 3 spent, cap 5. ROUNDS 4 AND 5 MUST BOTH BE CLEAN to converge exactly at the cap. If round 4 carries any valid finding, the loop reaches the cap without converging and ESCALATES TO THE HUMAN at round 5. The counters are NOT reset by this fix pass.

ROUND 3 RAN AND IS `new_valid` (2026-08-04, record 271): SIX valid findings, TWO `medium` and FOUR `low`, ZERO INVALID, and for the FIRST TIME IN THIS LOOP NO `high` IN THE RAW SET AT ALL, so no backstop was owed and nothing was downgraded out of that band. Streak still 0 of 2, round 3 of a cap of 5. THE SEVERITY CEILING HAS FALLEN, high then high then medium, which is the shape a converging artifact makes; the ZERO-INVALID count is the other signal, since rounds 1 and 2 each rejected findings and this one rejected none.

THE ROUND TURNED ON A DIRECT CONTRADICTION BETWEEN TWO LENSES, AND THE TRIAGER'S ANSWER WAS THAT NEITHER MISMEASURED. The fix-verification lens enumerated six layouts of the newly reachable two-root intersection and reported that NOTHING REFUSES WHAT IT DID NOT ALREADY REFUSE; the adversarial lens filed `R3A-1` saying a `--plan` naming a NONEXISTENT path MANUFACTURES a root that OVERRIDES the root from a `--source` that DOES exist, so A SINGLE PROJECT'S OWN log and ledger are withheld at exit 0. THE TRIAGER BUILT THE DISCRIMINATOR, one fixture and three controls against both binaries: C0 (no `--plan` supplied) reads alpha's own artifacts; C1 (`--plan` supplied, file missing) omits both; C2 (`--plan` supplied, file written) omits both. POST-FIX C1 EQUALS C2, which is the fix-verification lens's claim and is TRUE; POST-FIX C1 DIFFERS FROM PRE-FIX C1 AND FROM C0, which is the adversarial lens's claim and is ALSO TRUE. THE SIX LAYOUTS ALL DEFINE BOTH ANCHORS AND VARY ONLY WHETHER THE NAMED FILE EXISTS, so the dimension "is a `--plan` supplied AT ALL" is never crossed and C0 CANNOT APPEAR. THE LESSON IS SHARPER THAN ROUND 2'S AND SUPERSEDES NOTHING: A NEGATIVE RESULT IS BOUNDED NOT ONLY BY THE DIMENSIONS A FIXTURE VARIED BUT BY THE CONTROL IT COMPARED AGAINST, and this lens measured the three moved cells correctly and then adjudicated them against the plan-present control INSTEAD OF against the pre-fix behaviour it had just measured. THREE LENSES SAW THIS BEHAVIOUR AND ONE FILED IT: the acceptance lens built the same thing in its own part 3 and PRESENTED IT AS THE FIX WORKING.

`R3A-1`'S VALIDITY RESTS ON THE HUMAN'S OWN DECISION TEXT, NOT ON THE DIFFERENTIAL ALONE, WHICH IS WHAT MAKES IT MORE THAN A PREFERENCE. `Q-55-emptyroot` DECLINED the unpairable option expressly BECAUSE it "would omit an artifact legitimately belonging to the anchor's own directory", and THE BUILT FORM NOW DOES EXACTLY THAT for a supplied-but-missing anchor. The decision's stated narrowness, that "the only invocations whose output changes are ones currently leaking", is FALSIFIED, since pre-fix C1 leaked nothing. It is absorbed by NEITHER the in-root bound (disjoint siblings, and the direction is refusal rather than admission) NOR costs (iii) and (iv), both of which require a `--plan` THAT EXISTS via the parent-directory fallback that a nonexistent path never reaches.

THE FOLLOW-ON RULING MATTERS FOR THE `Q-55-ledgerdivergence` LINE OF WORK: THE SURFACE SPLIT `R3A-1` REPORTS IS NOT A NEW INSTANCE OF `ADV-1`'S CLASS, IT IS ITS MIRROR IMAGE. `ADV-1` was a surface with TOO FEW roots ADMITTING FOREIGN content; here `next`'s predicate FIRES and passes the project's OWN content while `status --resume` refuses. The adversarial lens's separate strictness proof is CONSISTENT with its own finding, verified by the triager by code reading and a twelve-configuration scan with no row where `resume` is looser: STRICTNESS RUNS ONE WAY ONLY, `status --resume` at least as strict as `next` and `status`, which is the SAFE direction and is already recorded as accepted cost (iv) on the existing-`--plan` population.

THE TRIAGER SPOT-CHECKED BOTH LARGE POSITIVE RESULTS AND ADDED A CONTROL THE REVIEWERS DID NOT RUN. `G-EMPTYROOT` is closed and guarded (reverting `src/main.rs:1516` reds exactly its own test); the central anchor-rooting mutation is caught on the same three tests all three rounds report; `R3F-1` reproduces exactly, with the deletion leaving the exact 416-passing baseline. ON ACCEPTANCE CHECK 11 IT RAN A CONTROL THE ACCEPTANCE LENS OMITTED, the same plan against its own ABSENT log, which exits 0 with "skipping the workflow check", PROVING THE REFUSAL IS WHAT PRODUCES THE NON-ZERO EXIT rather than some other failure. It also corrected one imprecision in that lens's table: on check 19b `validate --workflow` exits 1 only in the MARKDOWN spelling, while the TOML spelling exits 0, though the lens's narrative was correctly scoped to `status --resume`, which holds in both.

THREE THINGS ALL THREE LENSES MISSED, found by the triager: a THIRD "as spelled" claim site at `tests/unsafe_pairings_are_refused_and_omitted.rs:663` where `R3F-2` names only two; that `R3A-3`'s direction is a FALSE REFUSAL ON THE ANCHOR'S OWN LOG, which the pre-fix build read, so "the direction is safe" is only half true; and that `R3A-1` and `R3A-3` SHARE ONE CAUSE, the increment trusting a root derived from a path with nothing behind it.

ORCHESTRATOR DEFECT (14), MINE, CAUGHT AND FULLY RECOVERED BEFORE ANY REVIEWER SAW IT (2026-08-04). THE MECHANISM: the harness's shell keeps its WORKING DIRECTORY ACROSS CALLS, and the orchestrator had `cd`-ed into the implementer's worktree several calls earlier to verify the fix pass, so a later `cat >> docs/metrics/workflow.jsonl` written with a RELATIVE PATH appended the two `Q-55-emptyrootsite` and `Q-55-owedcount` receipts INTO THE WORKTREE'S copy of the metrics log rather than main's, and the `git add -A && git commit` that followed committed them ONTO THE REVIEWED BRANCH as `281d6c0`. Three review worktrees were then created at nested paths INSIDE the implementer worktree for the same reason. THE HARM THIS NEARLY DID, which is worse than the misplaced file: THE METRICS LOG WOULD HAVE BECOME PART OF THE REVIEWED INCREMENT'S DIFF, breaking the rule that the orchestrator owns the log and that it is not reviewed product, and the scope lock recorded for this loop measures `git diff main..HEAD -- docs/` as EMPTY, which would have silently become false. THE RECOVERY, verified at each step: the three misplaced worktrees and their branches removed, the two receipt lines moved to main's log, the branch `reset --hard` back to `4186ec2` and confirmed clean with its log back at 268 records, main revalidated at 270 records with invariants holding and both receipts present. NO REVIEWER EVER READ THE BAD STATE. THE STANDING CURE, and it is a discipline the file-safety rules do not currently state: THE ORCHESTRATOR WRITES TO MAIN WITH ABSOLUTE PATHS, OR `cd`s TO THE REPOSITORY ROOT IN THE SAME COMMAND, NEVER RELYING ON THE INHERITED WORKING DIRECTORY. The `Edit` tool was unaffected throughout because it was given absolute paths, which is why the ledger edit landed correctly while the shell append did not; that asymmetry is the tell. THIS IS ALSO EVIDENCE FOR THE FILE-SAFETY SECTION: the "clean tree before a writer" rule protects main from the WRITER, and nothing in it protects the reviewed branch from the ORCHESTRATOR.

THE ROUND 2 FIX PASS IS IN AND IT DEPARTED FROM A DECIDED PRESCRIPTION, CORRECTLY, WITH THE ORCHESTRATOR REPRODUCING THE COUNTERFACTUAL FIRST-HAND (2026-08-04, one commit `4186ec2`). AGAINST THE ROUND 2 TIP it is 245 added and 12 removed across four files; against main the increment stands at 2066 added and 106 removed. THE WHOLE PRODUCT CHANGE IS ONE CHANGED LINE, ONE NEW EIGHT-LINE FUNCTION AND TWO CALL SITES, with the rest doc comments and 170 lines of tests. Gates re-run by the orchestrator on the branch: 416 tests passing with 0 failures, clippy silent, validators green, `render --check` up to date, ASCII clean on all touched files, `git diff main..HEAD -- docs/` still EMPTY.

`Q-55-emptyrootsite` DECIDED (2026-08-04, receipt): CONFIRM THE DEPARTURE AS BUILT, over routing it back for the literal placement and over re-deciding the remedy from scratch. WHAT HAPPENED: `Q-55-emptyroot`'s option text named `canonical_project_root` (`src/main.rs:1327`) as the site, taking that location from the triager; the implementer measured that following it literally OPENS A NEW LEAK and put the change in `resume_roots` (`src/main.rs:1509`) instead, flagging the departure rather than making it silently. THE ORCHESTRATOR VERIFIED THIS ON ITS OWN FRESH FIXTURE rather than relaying, running the implementer's own literal-placement variant binary beside the pre-fix and post-fix builds: with `--source` naming an EXISTING Markdown-primary plan in alpha, `--plan` naming a NONEXISTENT file in beta, and `--metrics` naming beta's log, THE LITERAL PLACEMENT PRINTS `metrics: 3 records` AND READS THE FOREIGN LOG, while both the pre-fix binary and the built form REFUSE. THE CAUSE IS STRUCTURAL AND VISIBLE IN THE CODE: `canonical_project_root` serves TWO ROLES, and its `None`-on-nonexistent is LOAD-BEARING in one of them. `checked_plan_root` calls it on THE CHECKED PLAN, where `None` means "no plan was read"; `resume_roots` called it on THE ANCHORS, where that same `None` was dropped by a `filter_map` and produced the empty vector. Making it infallible therefore makes a `--plan` THAT DOES NOT EXIST count as a plan that WAS read, so containment roots on the missing plan alone and stops consulting the `--source` beside it. THE TRIAGER LOCATED THE CONCEPT CORRECTLY AND THE BYTE LOCATION WRONG, and the decision's intent, reuse `resolve_for_containment`, add no new concept and no new vocabulary, is exactly what was built. THE TRANSFERABLE RULE: A PRESCRIPTION THAT NAMES A CODE LOCATION IS A HYPOTHESIS ABOUT THAT LOCATION, NOT PART OF THE DECISION, AND AN IMPLEMENTER THAT MEASURES IT FALSE SHOULD DEPART AND SAY SO. Both halves of `Q-55-emptyroot` are pinned by separate tests: reverting the containment line reds one, reverting the human-line note reds the other.

THE FIX PASS ALSO DECLINED TO WRITE ONE CLAUSE THE TRIAGE PRESCRIBED, BECAUSE ITS OWN FIX MADE THAT CLAUSE FALSE, which is the calibrated claim rule applied by an implementer to a prescription for the first time in this task. Triage section 3.4 prescribed recording that "`status` and `next` structurally hold at most one root"; that was true ONLY WHILE a nonexistent `--plan` was being dropped, and post-fix a Markdown-primary `--source` that exists beside a `--plan` that does not gives them TWO. Writing the prescribed sentence would have shipped a FRESH FALSE CLAIM into the very paragraph the round exists to fix. The prose instead states the rule in the direction it holds.

`Q-55-owedcount` DECIDED (2026-08-04, receipt): FOLD INTO THE DOCUMENTATION-CURRENCY PASS AFTER INC3, over a planner pass now. THE ITEM: the sidecar's acceptance-check section states inc2 owes FOUR red-then-green demonstrations (checks 11, 13b, 14b, 14e), and the round 2 fix added a FIFTH genuine red case, the nonexistent-anchor test; the implementer could not edit a planner-owned sidecar and reported it. Chosen on the same principle as `Q-55-plandoccurrency`, that documentation currency does not widen a settled increment, and because THE COUNT UNDERSTATES THE EVIDENCE, which is the SAFE DIRECTION OF ERROR since it claims less than was delivered. THE ACCEPTED COST: a round 3 reviewer reading the sidecar as the specification may raise the mismatch, so it is recorded here where a triager will find it.

ROUND 2'S THREE ROUTED DECISIONS ARE TAKEN (2026-08-04, receipts `q_id:"Q-55-emptyroot"`, `q_id:"Q-55-fallbacksurface"`, `q_id:"Q-55-plandoccurrency"`).

`Q-55-emptyroot`: PARTIALLY RESOLVE THE ANCHOR, over treating "an anchor was supplied and no root could be derived" as unpairable and over accepting the leak as a fifth cost. THE FIX: apply the treatment `resolve_for_containment` (`src/main.rs:1350`) ALREADY PERFORMS, absolutise, canonicalise the longest existing ancestor, re-append the rest, to the ANCHOR in `canonical_project_root` (`src/main.rs:1289`), so a typo'd `<alpha>/docs/plans/q.plan.toml` yields the root `<alpha>` and the leak closes with NO NEW CONCEPT AND NO NEW VOCABULARY (One source of truth). THE REASONING PRESENTED. It is the NARROWEST of the three against Safe on existing projects: the only invocations whose output changes are ones CURRENTLY LEAKING or currently reading an artifact outside the anchor's project, and the DECIDED NEITHER-ANCHOR CASE IS UNTOUCHED, so the current-directory-relative paths `README.md:235` documents still stand. The unpairable option was declined as a WIDER behaviour change that would also omit an artifact legitimately belonging to the anchor's own directory, losing its own log for a run against a plan file not yet written, and that would need a new reason variant or a stretched `log-not-this-project`, REOPENING the settled `Q-55-jsonreason` vocabulary. Accepting it was declined because it would leave a `high` content-injection path live in the increment whose whole purpose is closing that class, against sidecar line 303's rule that passing this increment's own tests is not sufficient evidence for it. THE ACCEPTED WEAKNESS, STATED RATHER THAN TRADED AWAY SILENTLY: this option is the weakest of the three against Fail loudly, since the operator is still not told their anchor was a typo, SO THE FIX ALSO PRINTS THE ANCHOR-DOES-NOT-EXIST CONDITION ON THE HUMAN LINE, matching the `note:` shape `next` and `status` already use for a `--source` that does not parse.

`Q-55-fallbacksurface`: ABSORBED, FILE NOTHING, confirming the triager's ruling that `R2A-2` is INVALID for this increment. A Markdown-primary `--source` outside any `docs/plans` with no `--plan` now omits its own project's explicitly named log and ledger, measured as read at `HEAD~1` and omitted at `HEAD`; it is a NEW SURFACE of `project_root_of_source`'s fallback, and sidecar line 271 tells implementers and reviewers not to accumulate a fresh accepted cost on every new surface of exactly that fallback. The measurement is recorded against the QUEUED validation-constraints step, which already owns the shared cause. Recording it as a fifth accepted cost with a test beside check 19b was declined as a specification edit and a test for a population line 271 anticipated in writing (Minimal by default).

`Q-55-plandoccurrency`: LEAVE IT TO THE DOCUMENTATION-CURRENCY STEP AFTER INC3, over taking the one-line correction now. `Projection.plan`'s doc comment (`src/main.rs:570`) claims the field is "present only when a readable `--plan` was given", which is FALSE because a TOML-primary `--source` with no `--plan` populates it; the claim is PRE-EXISTING ON MAIN and this increment's own added sentence is true on its own terms. Folding the correction in would WIDEN A SETTLED INCREMENT, and the currency pass scheduled at the step close already carries four present-tense claims inc1 falsified plus whatever inc2 and inc3 falsify (sidecar line 293). THE COST ACCEPTED: a false doc comment stays on main for two more increments, and a writer agent reading it as ground truth is a stated risk in this project.

ROUND 2 RAN AND IS `new_valid` (2026-08-04, record 265): FIVE valid findings after dedup, ONE `high` and FOUR `low`, TWO INVALID, ZERO dismissed and ZERO downgraded out of the `high` band, so NO backstop is owed. Streak still 0 of 2, round 2 of a cap of 5. Three lenses on mixed models plus a separate triager, all cut from the branch tip.

THE ROUND'S ONE `high` IS `G-EMPTYROOT`, FOUND INDEPENDENTLY BY TWO LENSES ON THE SAME MODEL TIER AND DEDUPED TO ONE: when NO ANCHOR CANONICALISES, `containment_roots` returns an EMPTY VECTOR, `.iter().find(...)` is `None`, and the all-quantifier over an empty set is VACUOUSLY SATISFIED, so `status`, `status --resume` and `next` read a foreign explicit `--metrics` or `--ledger-fragment` and echo another project's `## RESUME STATE` at exit 0 with BOTH reason fields `null`, positively asserting the artifacts are this plan's. ONE CHARACTER CHANGED IN THE `--source` PATH flips a double refusal into the leak. The increment's own test for that path only ever writes an anchor that EXISTS, so nothing pinned it.

THE TWO LENSES DISAGREED ON CAUSE AND ON SEVERITY AND THE TRIAGER SETTLED BOTH BY MEASUREMENT. ON CAUSE, THE FIX-VERIFICATION LENS WAS RIGHT AND THE ADVERSARIAL LENS'S VERB WAS WRONG: the triager diffed its own fixture transcript between a `HEAD~1` build and a `HEAD` build and found EVERY EMPTY-ROOT CELL BYTE-IDENTICAL, with the only changed block being the control, so NOTHING WAS "RESTORED" because this configuration WAS NEVER CLOSED. What the adversarial framing gets right is that `containment_roots` is now the SINGLE PLACE the hole lives and ITS OWN DOC COMMENT POSITIVELY ASSERTS THE LEAK CANNOT HAPPEN. ON SEVERITY THE TRIAGER WENT UP, not down, calibrating fact by fact against round 1's `ADV-1`: identical payload, identical false `null` on the machine surface, identical exit 0, identical one-explicit-flag reachability, and THE ONLY DIFFERENCE IS THAT `ADV-1` LEFT `status --resume` HONEST WHILE THIS LEAVES NO HONEST SURFACE, which is a worsening rather than a mitigation. `run_resume` carries the hole independently at `src/main.rs:1475`, untouched by the fix pass, so all three surfaces are vacuous together.

THE DISCRIMINATING-CONTROL PROCEDURE ADDED AFTER ROUND 1 WORKED, AND IS NOW EVIDENCED RATHER THAN ARGUED. Every lens that touched the in-root bound ran the control and the triager re-ran it itself in BOTH arrangements: DISJOINT, the attack leaks and the control refuses; NESTED, both leak. THE VARIABLE IS THE ANCHOR, NOT THE NESTING, so `G-EMPTYROOT` is not the in-root bound and was filed rather than buried. Round 1's defect (13) is therefore CURED IN PRACTICE, not just recorded.

A REVIEWER'S OWN POSITIVE RESULT WAS FALSIFIED BY THE TRIAGER, WHICH IS THE MEASURED FIX-PASS RULE FIRING ON A REVIEWER FOR THE FIRST TIME IN THIS TASK. The fix-verification lens ran a 12-anchor by 3-artifact-set by 6-surface matrix, 216 invocations against both binaries, and concluded "NOTHING THAT WORKED BEFORE NOW REFUSES". THE ARITHMETIC IS FINE AND THE GENERALISATION IS NOT: the matrix varies WHICH ANCHORS EXIST but never WHERE THE SOURCE SITS relative to `docs/plans`, so the negative holds INSIDE the matrix and fails outside it, falsified by the other lens's `R2A-2` which the triager reproduced on this commit. THIS IS EXACTLY THE CALIBRATED PATTERN, a claim whose SCOPE IS WIDER THAN ITS EVIDENCE, and the lesson generalises past fix passes: A REVIEWER'S NEGATIVE RESULT IS BOUNDED BY THE DIMENSIONS ITS FIXTURE VARIED, AND MUST BE STATED WITH THOSE DIMENSIONS NAMED.

THE FIX-VERIFICATION LENS'S OTHER RESULTS SURVIVED A SIX-MEASUREMENT SPOT-CHECK BY THE TRIAGER: all NINE round 1 findings verified closed, ZERO regression across thirteen re-run mutations including the central anchor-rooting one on exactly its three named tests, the fix itself guarded by exactly one test, and a test diff of 214 insertions and ZERO deletions so no test's meaning changed. TWO INVALID FINDINGS, both ruled out on the increment boundary rather than on their facts, which were confirmed: `R2A-2` (a Markdown-primary `--source` outside any `docs/plans` with no `--plan` now omits its own project's explicit artifacts) is a NEW SURFACE OF `project_root_of_source`'s FALLBACK, which sidecar line 271 explicitly forbids accumulating a fresh accepted cost for; and `R2C-2` (`Projection.plan`'s doc comment claiming the field is "present only when a readable `--plan` was given", false because a TOML-primary `--source` populates it) is PRE-EXISTING ON MAIN, with the increment's own added sentence true on its own terms.

THE TRIAGER ALSO CORRECTED A REVIEWER'S PROVENANCE CLAIM, which changed a verdict rather than a wording: the claims lens called the offending README paragraph "pre-existing on `main`", and `grep -c` on `main:README.md` returns 0, so `R2C-1` is IN scope rather than out of it.

THE ROUND 1 FIX PASS IS IN, MEASURED BY THE ORCHESTRATOR ON THE BRANCH (2026-08-04, one commit `e71a795` on top of `e95f674`). AGAINST THE ROUND 1 TIP it is 258 added and 19 removed across five files, of which 214 ADDED LINES ARE TESTS, so the pass is overwhelmingly ADDITIVE EVIDENCE rather than authored logic. AGAINST MAIN the increment now stands at 1833 added and 106 removed. THE PRODUCT CHANGE IS SMALL AND SINGLE-SITE: a new `containment_roots` at `src/main.rs:1317-1340` consumed at three call sites, `run_status`'s log and `run_next`'s log and ledger, defined as `checked_plan_root(...).map_or_else(|| resume_roots(source, plan), |root| vec![root])` with each root tested through the SAME `is_outside_root` in the SAME call shape `run_resume` already used. THAT PRESERVES THE RULING THIS ROUND ESTABLISHED rather than inventing a third rule: where a plan is read the root still comes from that plan and the vector holds exactly one, so `Q-55-endproperty` is untouched, and where none is read the anchors supply it as `Q-55-resumepairing` already decided. The orchestrator re-ran the gates on the branch: 415 tests passing with 0 failures (up from 413), clippy silent under `-D warnings`, validators green at 264 records, `render --check` up to date, ASCII clean on all five touched files. `git diff main..HEAD -- docs/` remains EMPTY.

THREE THINGS THE FIX PASS RAISED AGAINST ITSELF AND ROUND 2 MUST JUDGE, recorded because the escape valve has now caught something in three consecutive dispatches. (1) IT AUTHORED PROSE THE TRIAGE DID NOT ASK FOR, which is precisely the class this project measures as re-seeding. `ADV-1`'s second aggravating fact was that it falsified `README.md`'s claim that "Every one of these commands checks that the log ... lives under the project root", and fixing the code left that sentence silent on the no-plan-read case, so the implementer added one identical clause to the README and the CHANGELOG naming that case. The numstat shows both files unchanged in total against main (README 23/2, CHANGELOG 2/0), so the edit lands INSIDE lines this increment already authored rather than adding a paragraph, but it is still authored prose in a fix pass and the round-2 claim lens is pointed at it. (2) `containment_roots` IS A NEW NAMED FUNCTION rather than an inline expression at three call sites; the implementer judged that naming a composition of two existing policy functions is not a third rule, and flagged that a reviewer reading the name otherwise would want three inline copies instead. (3) `validate --workflow` WAS DELIBERATELY NOT SWITCHED to `containment_roots`, and the asymmetry is argued rather than assumed: on that surface `checked_plan_root` returns `None` exactly when no plan is read, and that case already lands in the `--workflow` match's own `(None, None, _)` arm at `src/main.rs:1033` as a hard problem, so no configuration exists where `validate` reads a log with no root.

THE FIX PASS PROVED EACH CLOSURE BY RE-RUNNING THE TRIAGE'S OWN MUTATION, which is the standard this project now expects of a test-only finding: for each of `EVI-1` through `EVI-6` it applied the named mutation, observed the suite FAIL on the new assertion, reverted, and observed it pass. The `EVI-1` mutation re-opened the false pass visibly (`1 records, valid` then `workflow invariants hold`) before the new leaf-symlink test caught it. `ADV-1` carries a full red-then-green on a two-sibling fixture with no nesting: pre-fix `next` printed a foreign `## RESUME STATE` at exit 0 with `"resume_state_absent_reason": null`, post-fix it prints the rejection note with `"ledger-not-this-project"`, and `status --resume` on the same inputs is UNCHANGED, which is the point, since the two surfaces now agree.

THE `ADV-2` RESIDUAL IS UNAFFECTED AND THE IMPLEMENTER PROVED IT RATHER THAN ASSERTING IT: it built both of the triage's constructions and diffed the pre-fix and post-fix binaries' full output, byte-identical, then confirmed the comparison was genuine by checking the reverted binary still leaked the `ADV-1` block. Both constructions have a checked plan, so `containment_roots` returns a single-element vector and the path is unchanged.

ROUND 1'S ONE ROUTED DECISION IS TAKEN (2026-08-03, receipt `q_id:"Q-55-ledgerslot"`). `ADV-2`, VALID at `low`: under a divergent pairing `next` prints the REJECTED ledger path as the loop's `ledger:` context slot and, eleven lines later, prints that the same ledger was rejected, while on `--json` the qualifying note is dropped entirely because both note fields are `#[serde(skip)]`, leaving `context.ledger` unqualified beside a `resume_state_absent_reason` the consumer must think to correlate. CHOSEN: ACCEPT AND RECORD IT AS A RESIDUAL, over omitting the `ledger` slot when the ledger is unpairable and over naming what the CHECKED plan would resolve to instead. THE REASONING PRESENTED. It violates no written requirement, since the sidecar requires of an unsafe ledger only that the `RESUME STATE` echo be omitted and never mentions the context slot; the address it prints is ACCURATE, being the path `default_ledger_path` genuinely resolved under the decided source-first rule, so this is a wrong DESTINATION under a divergent pairing rather than borrowed CONTENT, and the human surface prints the contradiction in the same output; and the nearest precedent points the same way, since the sidecar already records `next`'s `review_findings` and `triage_findings` slots as unanchored under exactly this pairing, measured at work review on inc1 and deliberately owned by no increment here (Minimal by default, Ground decisions in evidence). THE OMIT OPTION WAS DECLINED because turning an always-present `ActiveLoop` slot into a sometimes-absent one is a JSON contract change that would want its own reason field by the same argument that produced `Q-55-jsonreason`, which is real scope on a `low` finding. THE RE-ROOT OPTION WAS DECLINED because it would name a ledger path the tool never opened and that may not exist, trading one misleading output for another (Make illegal states unrepresentable). SO `ADV-2` IS NOW A NAMED RESIDUAL BESIDE THE `review_findings` ONE AND MUST NOT BE RE-RAISED WITHOUT NEW EVIDENCE.

ROUND 1 RAN AND IS `new_valid` (2026-08-03, record 263): NINE valid findings after dedup, ONE `high`, FIVE `medium` and THREE `low`, ZERO dismissed, so NO backstop was owed. Streak 0 of 2, round 1 of a cap of 5. Three lenses on mixed models plus a separate triager on a fourth worktree, all four cut from the branch tip so citations resolve against the reviewed text. THE SHAPE OF THE ROUND IS THE OPPOSITE OF THE FIVE BEFORE IT AND THAT IS THE POINT: eight of the nine are TEST-ONLY or DOCUMENTATION findings and exactly ONE is a product defect, where the five plan-review loops on this step produced prose findings almost exclusively. The artifact changed kind, from a specification to the code that implements it, and the finding population changed with it.

THE ADVERSARIAL LENS FOUND THE ONLY PRODUCT DEFECT, WHICH IS THE SECOND CONSECUTIVE LOOP WHERE THE BREAK-IT LENS PAID FOR ITSELF. `ADV-1` (`high`): `next` echoes ANOTHER PROJECT'S `## RESUME STATE` verbatim at exit 0 on inputs where `status --resume` REFUSES THE SAME LEDGER, and on `--json` reports `"resume_state_absent_reason": null`, POSITIVELY ASSERTING the block is genuine. THE CAUSE, which the triager confirmed by independent construction: `checked_plan_root` short-circuits when NO PLAN IS READ, so both of `next`'s containment filters are VACUOUS, while `run_resume` supplies itself a root from the anchors via `resume_roots` exactly as `Q-55-resumepairing` decided. The suite missed it because the one test driving that case uses a TOML-primary source, which always yields a root.

THE ROUND'S DECIDING QUESTION WAS PUT TO THE TRIAGER AND NOT TO THE ORCHESTRATOR, and the answer was not the one either lens had. TWO lenses reproduced a `next` leak of the SAME VISIBLE SHAPE and attributed it to DIFFERENT CAUSES: the adversarial lens to the no-plan-read path, the conformance lens to the IN-ROOT BOUND, which is out of scope by human decision, so that lens filed NOTHING at all. THE TRIAGER BUILT A DISCRIMINATING CONTROL rather than arguing from the code: the same fixture files and the same command, run once with the inner project NESTED inside the outer and once moved to a DISJOINT sibling. Nested leaks both halves; disjoint refuses both. SO THEY ARE TWO DEFECTS, nesting alone accounts for the conformance lens's case, and its non-filing was CORRECT. ADV-1's construction uses two top-level siblings with no nesting anywhere, so nesting cannot be its cause.

THE `Q-55-ledgerdivergence` RESIDUAL IS NOW RULED ON, which discharges the obligation this loop inherited. THE RULING SURVIVES AT THE PRIMITIVE LEVEL: `is_outside_root` and `canonical_project_root` are genuinely shared and never duplicated per surface, so the sidecar's one-predicate claim holds. ADV-1 AMENDS IT RATHER THAN OVERTURNING IT: there is ONE PREDICATE AND TWO ROOT-SUPPLY POLICIES, and the second (`resume_roots`, decided by `Q-55-resumepairing` for "the surface that reads no plan") is INCOMPLETELY APPLIED, because `next` and `status` also read no plan in that configuration and never switch to it. RECORD THE AMENDED FORM, since the original ruling's sentence that the in-root bound is "the one place the two arities disagree" is FALSE: they also disagree on the no-plan-read case, which is disjoint-reachable.

A ZERO-FINDING LENS MISSED A LIVE DEFECT INSIDE ITS OWN REMIT AGAIN, WHICH IS THE SECOND CONSECUTIVE LOOP FOR THIS FAILURE MODE. The conformance lens gathered a real reproduction, attributed it correctly, and then generalised from it to a claim about where the two arities disagree WITHOUT CONSTRUCTING THE DISJOINT CASE that would have falsified the generalisation and handed it ADV-1. The recorded lesson stands and is now doubly evidenced: A CLEAN ROUND IS WORTH WHAT ITS LENSES ACTUALLY EXERCISED, NOT WHAT THEY COLLECTED. THE ORCHESTRATOR'S OWN CONTRIBUTION TO IT IS RECORDED AS DEFECT (13): telling three lenses that the in-root bound was out of scope was right for stopping rediscovery, and it also HANDED A REVIEWER A READY-MADE BUCKET to drop a live-looking reproduction into. The cure is not to drop the scope exclusion, which saved a round, but to REQUIRE A DISCRIMINATING CONTROL whenever a lens attributes something it reproduced to an out-of-scope cause, which is exactly what the triager was told to do and what settled the round.

THE TEST-EVIDENCE LENS RAN 29 MUTATIONS, 22 CAUGHT AND 7 SURVIVED, and its most valuable result is a NEGATIVE one: THE CENTRAL MUTATION IS CAUGHT. Re-rooting the predicate on the ANCHOR instead of the checked plan, which is the exact defect `Q-55-endproperty` exists to prevent, fails THREE tests including the two the sidecar names as separating the two rootings. The line-187 unsafe-is-not-absent trap is caught twice, at the CLI and in the pure function, and removing the withhold on any single surface fails a test. THE SURVIVORS CLUSTER IN THE REASON VOCABULARY AND MESSAGE TEXT, NOT IN THE PREDICATE'S VERDICT, with one exception, `EVI-1`: `resolve_for_containment` canonicalises the path ITSELF and no test reaches that clause, so changing `absolute.ancestors()` to `.skip(1)` leaves the whole suite green while re-opening a false pass on a log whose LEAF is a symlink out of the root. Both existing symlink tests symlink a DIRECTORY, never a leaf. THE TRIAGER RE-SEVERITISED IT DOWN from `high` to `medium` AND IT REMAINS VALID WITH A FIX OWED: the shipped code is correct at HEAD and the harm is prospective regression, so its remedy is one test rather than a code change.

REAL RED-THEN-GREEN, COUNTED HONESTLY: 12 of the 13 new integration tests are RED against the pre-fix product, and the thirteenth is a no-regression pin that correctly passes pre-fix and is NOT evidence for this increment. THE LENS ALSO DECLINED TO COUNT the 5 new unit tests as red-then-green evidence, because the three enums and the four `NextInputs` fields do not exist pre-fix, so their red is STRUCTURAL rather than behavioural. Recording that distinction because a round report would ordinarily smooth it over, and all four of the increment's owed demonstrations are genuinely red without needing it. THE SAME LENS CORRECTED THE ORCHESTRATOR'S BRIEF: it was told to expect `GOLDEN_HUMAN` to change, and it is UNCHANGED, correctly, because the retype maps each variant back to its historical string and the golden fixture has an active loop.

ALL EIGHT OF THE IMPLEMENTER'S SELF-RAISED JUDGEMENT CALLS WERE RULED SOUND, each by reproduction rather than by reading. Three are worth carrying. (c) The implementer read check 19's "a SYMLINK to a sibling directory" as a sibling of the ROOT; the triager ran BOTH readings and found that on the LOG side an in-root symlink target is NOT refused at all, so only the root-sibling reading can pin accepted cost (ii) there, while the plan side uses the sidecar's own in-root example and IS refused.
