THE ROUND'S RESULT IS ONE SYSTEMIC DEFECT, NOT TEN INDEPENDENT ONES, AND THE TRIAGER'S DIAGNOSIS IS THE MOST TRANSFERABLE THING THIS INCREMENT HAS PRODUCED. Six findings are HALF-FIXES: a round 1 remedy repaired one clause and left an adjacent clause in the same sentence carrying the same falsity. THE FIX PASS APPLIED EVERY PRESCRIBED EDIT EXACTLY and its escape valve caught the one misfit, so the defect is NOT in the writing. THE BOUND IS THAT EVERY REMEDY WAS A PER-SITE PRESCRIPTION DERIVED FROM A PER-SITE FINDING, so its reach was set by the REVIEWERS' ENUMERATION rather than by the CLASS. It has two spellings: the ENUMERATION bound (`R2A-4`, `R2B-2`, `R2B-3`, `R2C-3`) and the FORM bound already recorded as defect (17) (`R2A-1`'s numeral, `R2A-3`'s unnamed clause). THE GENERALISED RULE, which supersedes defect (17)'s narrower cure and must be applied to round 3's fix brief: WHEN A ROUND'S REMEDY IS A CLASS SWEEP, SCOPE THE BRIEF TO THE CLASS OVER THE WHOLE ENCLOSING SENTENCE AND PARAGRAPH, NOT THE QUOTED FRAGMENT, AND REQUIRE THE WRITER TO REPORT EVERY FURTHER SITE THE CLASS REACHES. A class-scoped sweep of the same six paragraphs would have closed all six.

ORCHESTRATOR DEFECT (18), MINE, AND IT MADE THINGS WORSE RATHER THAN MERELY MISSING SOMETHING. At round 1 the orchestrator took `R1C-6` on the triager's distinction between SUBJECT-MOVED and SUBJECT-REPLACED `src/checks.rs` citations and directed a narrowing of acceptance check 21b. THE DISTINCTION'S PREMISE IS FALSE: round 2 established that `owning_pid`, the citation round 1's triager called replaced and no longer existing, EXISTS AT `src/checks.rs:561`, alive and called, with its doc comment rewritten around `reserve_runner_worktree`. So ledger `:553` is false too and is corrected here by appendment. THE CONSEQUENCE MEASURED: `R1C-6` cost one re-pointed citation and a narrower disclosure, while the clause the orchestrator authorised leaves check 21b's post-condition UNMET FOR AT LEAST FIVE CITED RANGES and reopens the very scope `Q-55-currencyscope` closed. A `low` finding was answered with a `medium` one. THE STANDING CURE: AN ORCHESTRATOR MUST NOT RULE ON A TRIAGER'S FACTUAL PREMISE WITHOUT CHECKING IT, and the check here was one `grep` for a symbol name. The orchestrator verified the round 1 planner's re-pointed citations first-hand and did not verify the triager's claim that a symbol had ceased to exist, which is the asymmetry to fix.

THE ROUND 2 FIX PASS IS DONE at `0832f18`, AND THE CLASS-SCOPED BRIEF WORKED, WHICH IS THE FIRST TEST OF THE CURE THIS ROUND DIAGNOSED. The brief told the writer to identify each defect's CLASS, sweep the whole enclosing sentence and paragraph for it rather than the quoted fragment, fix every instance, and REPORT every further site the class reaches. IT FOUND AND FIXED TWO IN-PARAGRAPH INSTANCES NO FINDING HAD NAMED: `R2A-2`'s "and several of its named subjects no longer exist at all", refuted by its own two colon-examples since `nanos()` is at `src/checks.rs:1023` and the naming site is `reserve_runner_worktree` at `:498`; and `R2A-4`'s paragraph opening, where round 1 had re-tensed the two verbs BETWEEN the two ends and left both ends. THE TOTALS: 83 words deleted, 11 added, 13 tokens changed in place, and ZERO NEW FACTS AUTHORED, every added word being a tense auxiliary or a phrase copied verbatim from another site in the same file. THE WRITER ALSO VERIFIED TEN NAMED SUBJECTS IN THE COLLISION SIDECAR EXIST (`RUNNER_PREFIX`, `nanos`, `owning_pid`, `dead_pid`, `pid_is_alive`, `prune_orphan_worktrees`, `WorktreeGuard`, `RunError::WorktreeSetup`, `run`, `scratch`), which is the check round 1's triager did not run and which is the whole of defect (18).

`Q-55-spectime` DECIDED (2026-08-08, receipt record 292): COMPLETE THE SWEEP AND RE-TENSE THE WHOLE FAMILY, over declaring it spec-time and exempt, and over fixing only what contradicts. THE ITEM, raised by the round 2 fix pass's escape valve rather than by any reviewer: ABOUT TWENTY SENTENCES IN THIS SIDECAR ARE WRITTEN IN A SPEC-TIME FRAME, describing the tree as it was when the step was specified. They comprise the FOUR DEFECT STATEMENTS at `:5-9`, about FIFTEEN stale-"today" sites, and a family of IMPLEMENTER IMPERATIVES of which `:204`'s "the comment is the thing that is wrong" is the type case. All are false of the current tree and all were true of the tree they were written against. `R2A-3`'s own fix SHARPENED one of them into an outright contradiction: the `Q-55` record now asserts defect D CLOSED while the sidecar's head at `:8` still asserts it OPEN. THE GROUND FOR RULING THEM IN: they are already in scope under `Q-55-currencyscope`, because inc1, inc2 and inc3 are what falsified them, which is the condition the recorded out-of-scope precedent turns on; every edit is TOKEN CLASS with no new facts; no exemption has to be written down and defended, and writing one would mean narrowing an acceptance check, which is exactly what produced defect (18); this step's own check 21 already demands this treatment; and once the step is `complete` the sidecar becomes a record of what was done, for which the past tense is simply correct. THE COUNTER-ARGUMENT PUT AND DECLINED: no other plan step in this repository re-tenses its motivation after completing, so this invents a convention for one step, and past-tensing a Defects section can read as though the defects were never real.

`Q-55-impactlist` DECIDED (2026-08-08, receipt record 293): ADD THE MISSING BULLET, over deleting the exclusions bullet and over accepting a residual. THE ITEM: the `INC4:` documentation-impact list ENUMERATES ITS EXCLUSIONS ("NOT `README.md`, NOT `pack/AGENTS.md`, NOT `CHANGELOG.md`"), so it reads as exhaustive, while OMITTING `tests/unsafe_pairings_are_refused_and_omitted.rs`, which this increment edited under `Q-55-twinsites`. It is the one remedy in the round that authors a new bullet, roughly fifteen words, which is why the triager recorded the split rather than choosing. CHOSEN BECAUSE the fact it records is already settled and recorded, so nothing new is asserted, and because deleting the exclusions bullet instead would remove the text that pre-answers the obvious reviewer question of why no shipped prose changed, trading a known small defect for a likely new one.

`Q-55-check21b` DECIDED (2026-08-08, receipt record 291): REVERT THE ONE RE-POINT, over disclosing the single exception and over re-pointing all eleven. Deleting the clause defect (18) authorised is not in question and is prescribed by the triage; this decides what deleting it LEAVES BEHIND, because the deletion reopens `R1C-6`. THE CHOICE RESTORES `src/checks.rs:862-871` in `checks-runner-worktree-name-collision.md`, so no `src/checks.rs` citation was touched by this increment at all and check 21b's "AND ONLY THOSE" is TRUE with no clause needed. IT AUTHORS NOTHING, which is the property that matters after a round in which six of ten findings traced to remedies scoped too narrowly and one traced to a clause the orchestrator authored. IT ALSO MAKES THE FILE CONSISTENT: about eleven `src/checks.rs` citations there are stale for a cause this step did not create, so all eleven belong to the owning step TOGETHER rather than ten being stale and one arbitrarily current, and the one that was re-pointed was re-pointed only because it sat inside a sentence the planner was rewriting for a different finding, which is not a reason a reader can reconstruct. THE COST, STATED AND NOT HIDDEN: this deliberately restores a citation that is FALSE, inside a document whose purpose is removing false claims. It is defensible only because the alternative is an unexplained exception and because the owning step at order 93 is where every one of the eleven gets fixed.

`R2B-4` IS THE ONLY FINDING RULED OUT OF SCOPE, AND THE TRIAGER ARGUED IT AGAINST ALL FOUR CONDITIONS RATHER THAN ASSERTING IT. Acceptance check 19's SECOND layout, `<root>/docs/metrics` symlinked to an in-root sibling, is NOT refused: built with a clean record it gives `workflow invariants hold` AT EXIT 0 and `metrics: 1 records`, which is WORSE than the reviewer reported. The check is the odd one out, since the suite builds the OUT-of-root layout and says so in its comment and `:257`, `README.md:236` and `CHANGELOG.md` all state the general rule correctly. IT WAS NEVER TRUE, so no increment of this step falsified it and condition 3 holds; ruling it in would make condition 3 unsatisfiable for a documentation-currency increment and quietly retire the precedent. THE FIX IS RECORDED ANYWAY per the precedent's own guard, a token change from "to a sibling directory" to "out of the plan's project root", and the category is reported explicitly rather than as a bare clean.

`Q-55-w1figure` DECIDED (2026-08-08, receipt record 289): CORRECT THE `workflow-enforcement-tier-w1` WAIVER NOTE TO "13 valid findings (3, 4, 6)", over leaving it at 20 as a residual and over deleting the count. IT EXISTS BECAUSE OF AN ORCHESTRATOR DEFECT, (17), MINE, CAUGHT BY THE FIX PASS'S ESCAPE VALVE. THE DEFECT: the orchestrator transcribed the `Q-55-receiptcurrency` decision into the fix brief as "change only the TENSE of each of the eight claims", and put the same wording to the human as the option's own description, but the EIGHTH site has NO TENSE TO CHANGE. Its falsity is a NUMERAL that was never true, so the authorised action was inapplicable to the very site the triager had folded in as the eighth. THE PLANNER REFUSED TO READ IN AN AUTHORISATION and reported the conflict instead, which is the correct use of the valve and the THIRD time in this task a writer has caught an orchestrator brief. THE COST IT NAMED RATHER THAN LET THE ORCHESTRATOR DISCOVER: its own fix left the rendered plan asserting 13 in the risk paragraph and 20 in the waiver row, a twin-site disagreement the fix created. THE STANDING CURE, and it is narrower than the two already recorded: WHEN A BRIEF STATES THE FORM OF AN AUTHORISED EDIT, CHECK THAT FORM AGAINST EVERY SITE THE DECISION COVERS BEFORE PUTTING IT TO THE HUMAN, because a remedy class that fits seven sites can be inapplicable to the eighth. THE GROUND FOR THE CORRECTION: the append convention protects the decision-time REASONING, and this waiver's reasoning (the residual class is provably temporary) is untouched; 20 was never true, so this corrects an error rather than revising a judgement. The per-round breakdown restores the family convention its two siblings already keep, `-w2` at "24 valid findings (9, 5, 6, 4)" and `-w3` at "14 valid findings (6, 4, 2, 0, 2)", both of which the triager confirmed match the round log exactly, and a breakdown is self-checking against the log in a way a bare total is not. RECORDED DEFECT (14) RECURRED AS A NEAR MISS IN THE SAME MINUTE AND ITS CURE HELD, which is worth one line because it is the first evidence the cure works. The orchestrator's shell had inherited the implementer worktree as its working directory from a `git rebase` several calls earlier, so the VERIFICATION grep for this receipt ran against the worktree's copy of the log and reported ZERO HITS at 288 records, a FALSE NEGATIVE that invited re-applying an edit which had already landed. The Edit tool was unaffected because it was given absolute paths, which the original defect record names as the tell. Re-running with an explicit `cd` to the repository root in the same command gave 289 records and the receipt at line 289. IT THEN HAPPENED A THIRD TIME within the same loop, on the `Q-55-spectime` and `Q-55-impactlist` receipts, where the verification grep returned NOTHING and the `git commit` reported "nothing to commit" on `impl/wet-inc4` while the edits sat committed-pending on main. THE CURE AS ORIGINALLY RECORDED IS TOO WEAK BECAUSE IT IS STATED AS A CHOICE, "absolute paths OR a `cd` in the same command": the Edit-tool half is automatic and the Bash half has to be remembered, so the recorded rule is satisfied by the half that never fails and gives no protection to the half that does. THE STRENGTHENED FORM: EVERY orchestrator shell command that touches main PREFIXES `cd <repository root> &&`, unconditionally, including read-only verification, because a false negative from a verification command run in the wrong tree invites re-applying an edit that already landed.

A FOURTH OCCURRENCE FOLLOWED IMMEDIATELY AND IT RAN THE OTHER WAY, WHICH FALSIFIES THE CURE AS JUST STRENGTHENED. A SPAWNED WRITER, resumed into an existing worktree, found its own shell working directory pointing at THE MAIN REPOSITORY rather than at the worktree it was told to work in, so its relative-path commands were READING MAIN and would have WRITTEN THERE. It caught this from a branch check before making any edit, and `git status` on main confirms nothing landed. THE DIRECTION IS THE NEW INFORMATION: every previous occurrence was the orchestrator writing worktree-ward by accident, and the recorded remedy, "re-run with an explicit `cd` to the REPOSITORY ROOT", is exactly the wrong instruction for an agent that is already wrongly at the repository root. THE CORRECTED CURE, which covers both directions and is what the orchestrator now uses: NAME THE TARGET TREE EXPLICITLY IN EVERY COMMAND, by absolute path or by `git -C <tree>`, and NEVER `cd` to a bare root on the assumption that it is the right one. AN AGENT'S BRIEF MUST STATE ITS WORKTREE PATH AND REQUIRE A BRANCH CHECK BEFORE THE FIRST EDIT, which is what caught this one. THE UNDERLYING FACT, now evidenced four times in one task: THE SHELL WORKING DIRECTORY IS NOT DURABLE STATE AND MUST NEVER BE RELIED ON, by the orchestrator or by a spawned agent, and the Edit tool has been safe throughout only because it takes absolute paths.

ROUND 3 RAN AND IS `new_valid` ON IN-SCOPE FINDINGS ALONE (2026-08-08, record 294): TEN RAW from three lenses none of which ran before, TEN VALID AFTER DEDUP WITH ZERO DISMISSED, SIX IN SCOPE and FOUR OUT, seven `medium` and three `low`, nothing above `medium` so NO BACKSTOP IS OWED. Streak stays 0 of 2, round 3 of a cap of 5. The lenses were HISTORICAL-TRUTH (0 raw), STILL-TRUE-VERIFICATION (5 raw, 5 valid, all in scope) and MECHANICAL-DETECTABILITY (5 raw, 5 valid, 1 in scope).

THE HISTORICAL-TRUTH LENS RETURNED ZERO AND EARNED IT, which is the first zero-finding lens on this increment and the most expensive verification anyone has run on it. Instead of reading the past-tense claims it BUILT FOUR BINARIES FROM `git archive` SNAPSHOTS at the correct pre-fix commits and REPRODUCED THE DOCUMENTED DEFECT OUTPUTS: defect A's false green byte-matching at pre-inc3 `36e19f0`, defect B's borrowed-slug cross-project pass at pre-inc1 `69c0525`, defect C on both surfaces at `3170e3f`, and defect D's unqualified backstop sentence present verbatim pre-inc3 in both the source and a scaffolded fixture. It recomputed all three waiver figures independently, matching including severity ceilings. IT ALSO FOUND A TRAP THAT WOULD HAVE INVALIDATED THE EXERCISE and that any later archaeology on this repository must know: THE FAST-FORWARD MERGE COMMITS' IMMEDIATE PARENTS ARE MID-BRANCH, NOT PRE-INCREMENT BASELINES, so the obvious `<merge>~1` builds the wrong tree and yields confident wrong answers; it corrected course using the "docs: start/open ..." markers instead. AND IT RULED THE CONDITIONAL-PERFECT QUESTION the round 2 writer flagged: both counterfactuals are true and THE MOOD CHANGE IS REQUIRED, because a plain past tense would assert that the bad outcome actually happened when the work is what prevented it.

THE STILL-TRUE LENS GIVES THIS LOOP ITS FIRST REAL DENOMINATOR: 132 DISCRETE CLAIMS CHECKED, 127 CONFIRMED TRUE. That covers the round 2 writer's entire STATED retention set of 46 (45 true, 1 false) plus 86 unstated retentions, including all 38 line-numbered citations resolved mechanically, all 70 quotations matched whitespace-normalised, and eight calibration figures recomputed from the log. A clean round on this artifact is now worth something measurable rather than being an absence of complaints.

`R3B-1` REFINES THE ROUND 2 CURE AND THE TRIAGER PLACED THE DEFECT PRECISELY. Round 2's `R2B-1` was a finding whose REVIEWER NAMED THREE SITES; the TRIAGER'S MINIMAL REMEDY NAMED ONE; the orchestrator's brief said to work from the triage; the writer swept that one site's sentence and paragraph exactly as the class-scoped rule required. The other two sites sit in three different paragraphs and one in a different section, so A PARAGRAPH-SCOPED SWEEP COULD NEVER HAVE REACHED THEM AND THE WRITER APPLIED THE RULE CORRECTLY. `grep` for the two sites in the round 2 triage returns ZERO MENTIONS: they were DROPPED, not judged. THE TRIAGER DECLINED TO WIDEN THE SWEEP BOUND TO THE FILE, on the ground that that makes every remedy an authoring pass. THE RULE IT SET INSTEAD, which is the missing TRIAGER-SIDE half of the writer-side rule recorded above: A TRIAGER'S MINIMAL REMEDY MUST ACCOUNT FOR EVERY SITE THE FINDING'S REVIEWER NAMED, WITH AN IN-PLACE VERDICT FOR EACH DROPPED SITE, BECAUSE A SITE THAT APPEARS IN A FINDINGS FILE AND IN NO VERDICT IS A LOST FINDING. COMPANION RULE FOR THE ORCHESTRATOR: THE FIX BRIEF'S SITE LIST IS THE UNION OF THE TRIAGE'S REMEDY SITES AND THE SITES THE UNDERLYING FINDINGS NAMED.

THE DETECTABILITY LENS MEASURED THE INVERSION AND THE ANSWER IS ZERO OF TWENTY. None of the twenty in-scope findings from rounds 1 and 2 is catchable by any gate this repository owns. SIX OF SEVEN ARTIFACT MUTATIONS SURVIVED ALL GATES GREEN AND SILENT: a wrong `file:line`, a waiver figure changed from 13 to 20, a re-introduced present-tense claim, an inverted acceptance-check exit code, a HUMAN DECISION REVERSED BY DELETING ITS RECEIPT, and a falsified quotation of the tool's own output. Only a hand-edit of the generated view was caught, by `render --check --strict`, and that is a class NONE of the twenty belongs to. The positive control fired at exit 1, so the rig works. THE SHARPEST DETAIL: `validate --workflow` holds the waiver note claiming 20 and the round records summing to 13 IN MEMORY IN THE SAME RUN and reports `workflow invariants hold`.

THE STEP'S OWN THESIS SURVIVES, AND THE TRIAGER TESTED THAT RATHER THAN INHERITING THE REVIEWER'S RESTRAINT. It checked what each green ASSERTS: `workflow invariants hold` asserts W1 to W5 and the positive control fires when they fail; `95 steps, 69 questions, valid` asserts structural validity; `up to date` asserts copy consistency and the strict form fires on a hand-edit; and `pack/AGENTS.md:93` promises ROUND-LOG EVIDENCE, not prose truth. A FALSE GREEN REQUIRES A GREEN THAT ASSERTS SOMETHING UNESTABLISHED, and every green here is honest about its scope. Defect A was a check that DID NOT RUN reporting success, which is not this case. So the zero-of-twenty result is a gap in the project's MECHANISM COVERAGE, not a falsification of this step.

`R3C-4` WAS CORRECTED DOWNWARD BY THE TRIAGER, WHICH IS THE ADJUDICATION WORKING. The reviewer framed acceptance check 21 as "a check that cannot fail". The triager RAN IT: it IS executable, IS falsifiable, and FAILS RIGHT NOW at `:367`, all 13 line-numbered citations resolving in bounds and all 23 symbolic ones resolving by identifier presence. What survives is narrower and still valid: the check's headline claims coverage for occurrences its procedure does not define, and its "THIS FILE" scope misses the plan TOML sites where three of the twenty live. The triager measured both remedy facts FIRST-HAND BEFORE WRITING THEM, citing defect (18)'s standing cure by name.

FOUR FINDINGS ARE OUT OF SCOPE AND BECOME ONE BACKLOG STEP, THREE MECHANISMS, ordered by buildability: (1) a W6 check joining a waiver note's `<total> (<r1>, <r2>, ...)` breakdown against the `valid_findings` of the joined round records, where the convention already exists at three sites and only the enforcement is missing; (2) DANGLING-RECEIPT DETECTION, stage one being that every `q_id` must resolve to a registered question, which 29 of 51 fail today; (3) a QUOTATION RESOLVER, which automates what check 21 already instructs, with the recorded caveat that it would immediately go red on the roughly eleven `src/checks.rs` citations `Q-55-check21b` deliberately left stale. `R3C-5` goes to the brief convention rather than the step. THIS STEP IS PLANNER WORK AND IS OWED AFTER INC4 CLOSES, not during it.

`R3C-3` IS THE SAME QUESTION AS INC3'S TRANSFERABLE LESSON BUT A DIFFERENT DEFECT, and the triager confirmed `medium` rather than raising it. Inc3's was a mechanism that EXISTED, was NAMED as pinning the decision, and degenerated silently, which is a false green and cost six lines to fix. This is a mechanism NEVER CLAIMED: `pack/instrument.md:9` scopes W4 to registered `[[question]]` ids past the baseline. THE SHARPENING THE REVIEWER MISSED: all 29 unregistered `q_id`s are `Q-55-*`, one question's SUB-ID CONVENTION INVENTED BY THIS STEP. Against the cost, the log is append-only under git, the ledger narrates all 29, and the 22 registered ids are joined by W4.

ROUND 4 RAN AND IS `new_valid` ON IN-SCOPE FINDINGS ALONE (2026-08-08, record 297): SIX RAW from three lenses none of which ran before, SIX VALID WITH ZERO DISMISSED, FOUR IN SCOPE and TWO OUT, two `medium` and four `low`, no severity corrected in either direction and NO BACKSTOP OWED. Streak stays 0 of 2, round 4 of a cap of 5. The lenses were ACCEPTANCE-CHECK-EXECUTION (2 raw, 1 in-scope valid), CROSS-ARTIFACT-CONSISTENCY (4 raw, 3 in-scope valid) and COLD-COMPLETE-READ (0 raw).

`Q-55-classsweep` DECIDED (2026-08-10, receipt record 304): SWEEP THE CLASS TO EXHAUSTION, over closing only the six enumerated sites and over stopping and recording. THE GROUND: three successive authorisations had each closed the sites SOMEBODY HAD ENUMERATED and left the CLASS open, which is why it kept returning; only a sweep bounded by the class rather than by a list ends it. THE SWEEP CLASSIFIED 61 CANDIDATES, EDITED 9, STOPPED ON 6 AND EXCLUDED 46, with both search frames re-run afterwards returning ZERO UNCLASSIFIED LINES. ITS SECOND FRAME EARNED ITS PLACE AND THIS IS THE METHOD LESSON: a deictic frame (`plan's`, `this plan`, `that plan`) misses attribution phrased WITHOUT a deictic, and a second frame on the containment vocabulary (`vouch`, `belong`, `paired`, `project root`, `outside`, `anchor`) surfaced FOUR SITES THE FIRST DROPPED, THREE OF THEM FALSE. A single-frame sweep would have reported clean with those standing. THE SHARPEST FIND IS `src/next.rs:105`: round 4's `f56879c` deleted the false clause from the FIRST HALF of a sentence and LEFT "that plan" STRANDED IN THE SECOND HALF, ONE LINE DOWN, INSIDE THE SAME SENTENCE. The twin-site defect recurred WITHIN A SINGLE DOC COMMENT. THE SWEEP'S OWN STATED BOUND, recorded because it is what makes the result honest: it is a LEXICAL search, so a claim attributing containment to the plan without any of the eleven vocabulary items or the word "plan" would appear in neither frame; the sweep is complete with respect to its two frames, NOT with respect to every possible phrasing, and with no rounds left nobody will re-derive it.

`Q-55-changelog` DECIDED (2026-08-10, receipt record 305): NO ENTRY, AND DO NOT RE-HOME THE EXCLUSION, over adding a `Fixed` entry and over deferring the question to the inheriting step. THE ITEM, RAISED BY THE CLOSING PLANNER AGAINST A DECISION THE HUMAN HAD ALREADY TAKEN: receipt 295 (`Q-55-helpsurface`) declined a CHANGELOG entry on the explicit ground that one "would CONTRADICT the impact list's argument rather than qualify it", and the bullet carrying that argument is what `Q-55-impactclaim` then deleted, SO THE STATED GROUND NO LONGER EXISTS. Meanwhile ONE corrected `--help` string had become FIVE user-visible strings, two of which print at runtime. CHOSEN because receipt 295's SUBSTANTIVE ground survives untouched and covers all five: every one said something FALSE and now says something TRUE, with no behaviour altered, which the implementer verified byte-identical before and after, so these are documentation fixes rather than behaviour changes. AND THE EXCLUSION IS DELIBERATELY NOT RE-HOMED: a statement about what an increment does NOT touch IS A COMPLETENESS CLAIM, so re-homing it would rebuild the exact defect `Q-55-impactclaim` removed, and the sweep authorised in the same hour falsified it again immediately.

`Q-55-lastsix` DECIDED (2026-08-11, receipt record 306): RECORD THE SIX, CLOSE THE INCREMENT, over applying all six and over applying only the self-contradiction. THE ITEM: the class sweep closed nine sites by deletion and STOPPED ON SIX THAT NEED WRITTEN PROSE rather than a deletion or a conjunction. CHOSEN because every one of this increment's five rounds of findings came from prose somebody wrote, which is the most consistent pattern in this loop's calibration data, and writing six unreviewed sentences on a pass with NO ROUNDS LEFT is the highest-risk act available; the sweep had already removed every claim removable safely. THE COST ACCEPTED AND RECORDED PLAINLY: `src/main.rs:1725` SHIPS CONTRADICTING `src/main.rs:1716` NINE LINES ABOVE IT, since `:1716` states the anchor case correctly while `:1725` says the round evidence "must be the plan's own", so `run_next` now disagrees with itself in two adjacent comments. THE OTHER FIVE ARE `src/main.rs:1631-1635` (a third list member whose removal also needs a following sentence deleted and a verb form changed), `src/main.rs:1381` (`checked_plan_root` over-claiming as a universal, self-corrected four lines later at `:1408`), and `tests/unsafe_pairings_are_refused_and_omitted.rs:622`, `:737` and `:1106`. ALL SIX ARE MEASURED, NOT SUSPECTED, and they are the inheriting step's starting evidence.

AN ORCHESTRATOR BOUNDARY CALL, TAKEN AND RECORDED: the sweep edited a claim inside `src/next.rs:162`, a sentence whose OTHER claim ("Every derived part is optional") is on the declined list. THE EXCLUSION IS CLAIM-SCOPED, NOT SENTENCE-SCOPED: leaving that claim uncorrected while its exact twin at `src/main.rs:563` was corrected would have REBUILT THE TWIN-SITE DEFECT DELIBERATELY. The declined claim itself is untouched. THE SWEEP ALSO SUPERSEDED WORDING THE ROUND 5 TRIAGE HAD PRESCRIBED VERBATIM, and flagged it rather than hiding it: the triage was closing an ENUMERATED SITE while `Q-55-classsweep` bounds the pass BY THE CLASS, so the trailing "with this plan" went from the printed note entirely. No test pinned it.

`Q-55-stoppedsites` DECIDED (2026-08-10, receipt record 302): APPLY ALL FOUR, DELETION PLUS ONE CONJUNCTION, over applying only the three deletions and over recording all four. THE ITEM, raised by the closing implementer's escape valve rather than by any reviewer: FOUR FURTHER SITES IN THE SAME CLASS AS `R5A-1`, ALL MEASURED FALSE RATHER THAN SUSPECTED. It built a fixture where a Markdown-primary `--source` resolves no plan and observed the tool return POPULATED METRICS AND RESUME STATE ANYWAY, so presence is decided against the ANCHOR root with NO PLAN READ, which falsifies `src/main.rs:573` ("present only when the metrics log exists AND is this plan's"), `src/next.rs:176-177` (the same shape), `src/next.rs:184-185` (false in the OTHER direction, since the foreign-ledger run withholds on the anchor root rather than on any plan's), and `src/main.rs:461`, a `--help` STRING AN OPERATOR READS. THE COMPLICATION THAT MADE IT A DECISION: `src/next.rs:184-185` CANNOT BE CLOSED BY DELETION, because removing the clause leaves a two-item list with no conjunction, so it needs one word written with NO REVIEW ROUNDS LEFT TO CHECK IT. CHOSEN because three are pure deletions, the fourth writes a single conjunction carrying no claim of its own, the implementer had already specified the exact edit for each so the wording is not a fresh judgement, and one of the four tells an operator something false, which is the same class as the finding this whole escalation turned on.

`Q-55-impactclaim` DECIDED (2026-08-10, receipt record 303): DROP THE COMPLETENESS CLAIM AND KEEP THE BULLETS, over completing the list properly and over recording it as a residual. THE ITEM, RAISED BY THE CLOSING PLANNER AGAINST THE CLOSING FIX ITSELF: the implementer's commit landed AFTER the round 5 triage was written and SUBSTANTIALLY WIDENED THE INCREMENT'S EDITED-SITE SET, so the `INC4:` documentation-impact list is now short by far more than the one site `R5B-1` reported, and one of its sentences HAS BECOME FALSE, saying inc4 corrects ONE user-visible string where FIVE are now corrected. IT WAS TRUE BEFORE THE FIX AND IS FALSE AFTER IT. THE REMEDY DELETES the sentence counting user-visible strings and the exclusions bullet that makes the list read as exhaustive, KEEPING EVERY EXISTING BULLET, so nothing informative is lost and what goes is the promise the list cannot keep. THE GROUNDS: it applies this task's OWN STANDING CURE for orchestrator defect (12), "prefer no count at all over a maintained one", to the same failure it was written for; it is DELETION CLASS on a pass with no review rounds left, where completing the list would need several authored sentences; and IT ENDS THE RECURSION STRUCTURALLY, since this list produced a finding in rounds 4 and 5, both times because it enumerates, and every further edit to the increment falsifies it again. THE GENERAL LESSON, which outlives this step: A DOCUMENTATION-IMPACT LIST THAT ENUMERATES ITS OWN EXCLUSIONS IS A COMPLETENESS CLAIM, AND A COMPLETENESS CLAIM ABOUT AN INCREMENT STILL BEING EDITED CANNOT BE KEPT TRUE BY ANY AMOUNT OF DILIGENCE.

THE ESCALATION IS TAKEN (2026-08-10, records 300 and 301, receipt `q_id:"Q-55-inc4escalation"`). CHOSEN: AUTHORISE THE FIX, THEN MERGE, over re-scoping the source-side surface first and over applying the fix and resuming the loop with reset counters. THE COUNTERS THEREFORE RETIRE WITH THE LOOP rather than resetting, which is the branch the convergence rule specifies for "send it back for a specific fix that closes this loop"; the reset option was put explicitly and declined. THE AUTHORISED FIX IS ALL FIVE ROUND 5 REMEDIES, CONSTRAINED TO DELETIONS AND TOKENS EXCEPT THE ONE TWELVE-WORD BULLET that `R5B-1` requires. NOTHING ELSE IS AUTHORISED.

THE REASONING PRESENTED, recorded because it is what makes the choice checkable rather than a preference. FOR authorising the fix: NOTHING IN FIVE ROUNDS REACHED `high` OR `critical` AND NO ROUND EVER FOUND A DEFECT IN WHAT THE CODE DOES, every finding across all five being a false claim about it (Ground decisions in evidence); FOUR OF FIVE REMEDIES AUTHOR NOTHING, and this project has six measurements that deletion re-seeds nothing while authoring does, so this is the fix class least likely to manufacture a sixth round (Minimal by default); and it CLOSES THE NOT-ACTUALLY-CLOSED ROUND 4 FINDING rather than shipping a known-false string to an operator. AGAINST RESUMING THE LOOP, WHICH WAS NOT REJECTED ON MERIT: it honours the two-clean-round bar rather than short-circuiting it, and round 5 is precisely the round that proved the artifact still yields findings when a genuinely new lens is pointed at it; it was declined because fifteen reviewer passes have run, the exhaustion audit's plan-document predictions landed exactly, and the marginal round costs four agents to find `low` findings in text the last fix touched. AGAINST RE-SCOPING FIRST: it would answer the question the triager says was never asked and would recategorise three of five findings, but it re-opens a boundary five triage rulings have already applied, and it means shipping two false user-visible strings under a routing decision rather than deleting four words.

THE RESIDUAL ACCEPTED WITH THIS DECISION, STATED SO IT IS NOT DISCOVERED LATER: ACCEPTANCE CHECK 21 HAS PRODUCED A FINDING AFTER EVERY ONE OF THE FIVE TIMES IT WAS EDITED, UNBROKEN, so `R5B-2` touching it a sixth time is the known risk of this fix and was put to the human as such.

THE SCOPE-DRIFT QUESTION IS RECORDED AS OPEN AND DELIBERATELY NOT ANSWERED HERE. Inc4's subject grew past the boundary `Q-55-currencyscope` drew, one triage ruling at a time, each individually defensible on condition 3 of the out-of-scope precedent and none wrong on its own. THE CUMULATIVE EFFECT WAS NEVER PUT AS A QUESTION, and the human chose to close the increment rather than re-open the boundary. A LATER STEP THAT INHERITS THE SOURCE-SIDE CLAIM SURFACE SHOULD KNOW THIS HISTORY, because the precedent that admitted each widening is still binding and will admit more.

ROUND 5 RAN AND IS `new_valid` (2026-08-09, record 299), AND THE LOOP HAS REACHED THE CAP. SIX RAW from three lenses none of which ran before, FIVE VALID and ONE DISMISSED, ALL FIVE IN SCOPE, three `medium` and two `low`, nothing above `medium` so NO BACKSTOP IS OWED. One severity corrected DOWNWARD. Streak 0 of 2 at round 5 of a cap of 5, so THE LOOP ESCALATES. The lenses were SOURCE-SIDE-CLAIM-SWEEP (4 raw, 3 valid), FIX-PASS-RESIDUE (2 raw, 2 valid) and LENS-EXHAUSTION-AUDIT (0 raw). A DELETIONS-AND-TOKENS-ONLY FIX PASS CLOSES FOUR OF THE FIVE, nine of twelve sites; only `R5B-1` needs authored prose, about twelve words.

`R5A-1` IS THE MOST SERIOUS THING THIS ROUND CONTAINS: A ROUND 4 FINDING IS NOT CLOSED, AND THE REASON IS A FALSE PREMISE IN THE ROUND 4 TRIAGE. Round 4's `R4B-1` fix deleted the false plan attribution from two `src/next.rs` doc comments, but TWO USER-VISIBLE STRINGS at `src/main.rs:1509` and `:1522` state the same falsehood to an operator, and one of them is THE SOLE OUTPUT of `status --resume`, a surface that BY DESIGN NEVER READS A PLAN. The round 4 triage recorded "no user-visible string is wrong" as part of its ground, reproduced verbatim at `r4-triage:150`, and a two-line grep refutes it. It changed no verdict, since `medium` was confirmed anyway, BUT IT SCOPED THE FIX BRIEF, so the fix pass had no reason to look there. THE ROUND 5 TRIAGER WENT FURTHER THAN THE REVIEWER on its own evidence: it built a binary from `269d075^` and established that THE LEDGER HALF WAS BORN FALSE RATHER THAN FALSIFIED, `resume_roots` having arrived in `8beb1c2` itself, and it CORRECTED THE REVIEWER'S PRESCRIBED TEST-SPLIT RULE, whose discriminator is stdout versus stderr rather than the path prefix the reviewer named, a rule that would have left one site red.

`R5B-1` IS NOT RE-SEEDING, WHICH MATTERS BECAUSE IT LOOKS EXACTLY LIKE IT. The `INC4:` impact list was amended by round 4's fix SPECIFICALLY to stop it reading as exhaustive while omitting sites, and round 5 found it still omitting one. THE TRIAGER ESTABLISHED THE SITE WAS ALREADY MISSING AT `6ec1955`, BEFORE ROUND 4, and that round 4's own reviewer file records why: it counted the exclusions-bullet mention as coverage. Round 4's authored bullets are all accurate; THE ENUMERATION BEHIND THEM WAS SHORT BY ONE. Severity corrected `medium` to `low` on that ground.

THE LENS DISAGREEMENT IS ADJUDICATED IN FAVOUR OF THE SOURCE-CLAIMS LENS, AND THE REASON GENERALISES. The exhaustion audit put the remaining unexamined space at eight sidecar lines plus four in `src/next.rs` and predicted another round would most likely find nothing. ITS PREDICTIONS ABOUT THE PLAN DOCUMENTS LANDED EXACTLY, both `R5B-2` at `:345` and `R5B-1` at `:385-389`, both `low`, both in the sites it named. BUT ITS FRONTIER HAD NO ROW FOR THE SOURCE-SIDE SURFACE AT ALL, because it was built from twelve PLAN-DOCUMENT passes' self-reports, and A REVIEWER DOES NOT DISCLAIM A REGION IT NEVER FRAMED. The audit named that bound itself and did not carry it into its conclusion. THE TRANSFERABLE FORM: A COVERAGE AUDIT BUILT FROM REVIEWER SELF-REPORTS CAN ONLY SEE THE COMPLEMENT OF WHAT SOME REVIEWER THOUGHT TO MENTION, so it measures the examined space accurately and the unexamined space not at all.

THE ORCHESTRATOR'S DIAGNOSIS WAS CORRECTED TWICE, AND THE CORRECTED FORM IS THE CALIBRATION RESULT OF THIS LOOP. The orchestrator claimed each round's findings came from a lens type never run before. THE AUDIT SHOWED THAT IS HALF RIGHT: round 2's cold read was ITSELF A REPEAT of round 1's completeness lens, stating the identical method, and produced FOUR of round 2's nine findings on a line BYTE-IDENTICAL between the two trees, so a repeat lens has found material here. THE TRIAGER THEN CORRECTED THE AUDIT: every transcript, md5 and touched-line set reproduces, and the attribution table checks out, BUT "AT A RISING RATE" IS FALSE. The strict rates are 44.4, 33.3 and 50.0 percent and the inclusive ones 55.6, 50.0 and 50.0, which fall then rise or fall then flatten, while the ABSOLUTE COUNTS FALL 4, 2, 2. THE DEFENSIBLE STATEMENT IS A STABLE THIRD-TO-HALF SHARE OF A SHRINKING POPULATION. Separately, ACCEPTANCE CHECK 21 HAS NOW PRODUCED A FINDING AFTER EVERY ONE OF THE FIVE TIMES IT WAS EDITED, unbroken, which is the single sharpest instance of the authoring-re-seeds pattern this project has recorded.

THE QUESTION THE TRIAGER FLAGGED AND NOBODY HAS PUT TO THE HUMAN, WHICH THE ESCALATION MUST CARRY: INC4'S REAL SUBJECT HAS GROWN PAST THE BOUNDARY `Q-55-currencyscope` DREW, ONE TRIAGE RULING AT A TIME. Each widening was individually defensible on condition 3 of the out-of-scope precedent, and no single ruling was wrong, but the cumulative effect was never put as a question. IF THE SOURCE-SIDE CLAIM SURFACE BELONGS TO A LATER STEP, THREE OF THIS ROUND'S FIVE FINDINGS CHANGE CATEGORY.

`Q-55-round5` DECIDED (2026-08-09, receipt record 298): FIX ROUND 4, RUN ROUND 5, AND ESCALATE WITH ITS RESULT, over escalating immediately and over reclassifying the increment to converge. THE ARITHMETIC PUT TO THE HUMAN, WHICH IS DETERMINISTIC: with the streak at 0 of 2 and four rounds spent against a cap of 5, ROUND 5 ESCALATES WHATEVER IT RETURNS, because even a wholly clean round 5 gives a streak of 1 against a required 2 while reaching the cap. So the only question was whether to spend a round before that conversation. CHOSEN BECAUSE THE ESCALATION DECISION IS WHETHER TO ACCEPT THE INCREMENT, AND A CLEAN ROUND 5 IS MATERIALLY DIFFERENT EVIDENCE FOR THAT THAN NO ROUND 5: it would mean the artifact survived a fresh sampling after the final fixes, and round 4's own cold read proves a repeated lens on a changed artifact can genuinely return zero. THE THIRD OPTION WAS PUT AND DECLINED ON PRINCIPLE, and the reasoning is recorded because it is the exact bias the convergence rule exists to prevent: reclassifying `risky` to `low_risk` is the only path that converges without an escalation, the classification is a RECORDED PROPERTY FIXED AT LOOP-OPEN precisely so it cannot be revisited under convergence pressure, and this loop's own evidence supports `risky` rather than undermining it.

THE CALIBRATION RESULT THIS LOOP HAS PRODUCED, STATED BEFORE THE ESCALATION SO IT IS NOT CONSTRUCTED AFTERWARDS TO FIT THE DECISION: findings went 11, 9, 6, 4 across four rounds, and THE REASON THE COUNT HAS NOT REACHED ZERO IS THAT EACH ROUND'S FINDINGS CAME FROM A LENS TYPE NEVER RUN BEFORE. Round 4's cold read, DELIBERATELY REPEATING round 2's strongest lens on a much-changed artifact, found NOTHING while sweeping 100 percent of the text. BOTH of round 4's `medium` findings came from the CROSS-ARTIFACT lens, the first ever pointed BETWEEN artifacts rather than at one, and `R4B-1` sits in `src/next.rs`, a file NO LENS IN FOUR ROUNDS HAD OPENED. THIS ARTIFACT IS NOT FAILING TO CONVERGE BECAUSE FIXES RE-SEED; IT IS FAILING BECAUSE THE SPACE OF LENSES IS NOT EXHAUSTED. That is a different diagnosis from step 92's, where three of six rounds were self-inflicted by authoring fix passes, and it should change how a future loop budgets: the marginal round is worth what its NEW lens can reach, not what another pass of the same lenses would.

THIS ROUND PRODUCED THE LARGEST DENOMINATORS THE LOOP HAS SEEN, AND THE TRIAGER SAMPLED RATHER THAN ACCEPTED EACH. THE FULL ACCEPTANCE-CHECK LIST WAS RUN FOR THE FIRST TIME: 33 CHECKS, 33 RUN, 33 PASS, ZERO FAIL, ZERO UNRUNNABLE, including ALL 21 RED HALVES against binaries built from the TRUE pre-increment parents `1dac3dc`, `285a6a3` and `5684b5f`. Check 13b's red is the strongest form available: the pre-inc2 build printed `workflow invariants hold` AT EXIT 0 against an ANCHOR-ROOTED binary, which is exactly the false green `Q-55-endproperty` exists to defeat. The cross-artifact lens MAPPED 71 MULTIPLY-STATED FACTS AND CONFIRMED 67 AGREEING. The cold read SWEPT 100 PERCENT of the sidecar, the `Q-55` record, four increment entries and three waiver notes and found NOTHING. THE TRIAGER BUILT ITS OWN HISTORICAL BINARY at `5684b5f` to spot-check the check runner and reproduced check 15's red verbatim.

A TRAP IN THIS PROJECT'S OWN VERIFICATION METHOD WAS CAUGHT AND MUST BE CARRIED FORWARD, because historical-binary measurement has now been used by three separate lenses across this task. BUILDING SEVERAL `git archive` EXTRACTS INTO ONE SHARED `CARGO_TARGET_DIR` SILENTLY REUSES A STALE FINGERPRINT AND PRODUCES BYTE-IDENTICAL BINARIES: the check runner's pre-inc2 and pre-inc3 builds came out identical, so every "before inc3" measurement would in fact have been an "after inc2" one, with nothing in the output showing it. IT WAS CAUGHT BY `md5sum` and fixed with ONE TARGET DIRECTORY PER REVISION plus a `touch` of the sources. THE OTHER RECORDED TRAP STILL APPLIES ALONGSIDE IT: the fast-forward merges' immediate parents are MID-BRANCH, not pre-increment baselines.

`R4B-1` IS THE FIFTH TWIN-SITE DEFECT AND IT LANDED ONE ROUND AFTER A HUMAN DECISION CLOSED THE SAME CLAIM TWO SITES AWAY. `Q-55-reasondefs` deleted "of the plan this surface reads" from the sidecar at `:217` and `:229`; THE IDENTICAL CLAUSE SURVIVES AT `src/next.rs:105` AND `:140`, which are THE ONLY DEFINITIONS OF TWO SERIALISED CONTRACT TOKENS ANYWHERE IN THE SOURCE. The triager reproduced it and VERIFIED THE FALSIFIER RATHER THAN THE CITATION: `269d075`, an inc2 fix-round commit whose own message says both filters went vacuous before it, so condition 3 fails on its second limb and the finding is IN SCOPE on the same provenance `R3B-3` was ruled in on. THE TRIAGER FOUND ONE SOFT SPOT IN THE CLEAN DENOMINATORS AND REPORTED IT AGAINST ITS OWN CONCLUSION, which is the posture that makes the rest credible. THE CROSS-ARTIFACT LENS RECORDS THE PROJECT-MEDIAN FIGURE AS AGREEING OVER 85 ARTIFACTS WHILE THE COLD READ COMPUTED 175 ARTIFACTS AND A MEDIAN OF 1 AND DECLINED TO REPORT, its stated ground being that the `artifact` field's TEXT CHANGES BETWEEN ROUNDS of one loop, so grouping by it splits genuine multi-round series into spurious single-round entries. THE ROUND 3 FIX PASS IS DONE at `732376f` and BOTH NEW SITE-SCOPING RULES EARNED THEIR PLACE. Rule (a), that the fix brief's site list is the UNION of the triage's remedy sites and the sites the underlying reviewers named, made the writer open the reviewer files as well as the triage; on `R3B-1` the reviewer named a third site the triage had not, and the writer checked it, found it NOT false, and said so. ACCEPTANCE CHECK 21 WAS MEASURED BEFORE AND AFTER, WHICH IS THE FIRST TIME THIS INCREMENT'S OWN CHECK HAS BEEN USED AS A DETECTOR RATHER THAN DESCRIBED. `Q-55-reasondefs` DECIDED (2026-08-08, receipt record 296): CLOSE THEM NOW, over leaving them for round 4 and over closing them while also widening the sweep bound to whole-file. THE GROUND FOR CLOSING THEM: they are the same class as a finding already ruled valid, in the same sidecar, falsified by this step's own inc2, so they sit squarely inside item (1) of the scope the human set; they are MEASURED false rather than suspected; and the human had already answered this exact shape at `Q-55-spectime`, choosing to complete a sweep the writer reported rather than let the next round rediscover it. SHIPPING A DEFECT THAT CAN BE NAMED AND REPRODUCED INTO A REVIEW ROUND GUARANTEES THAT ROUND IS NOT CLEAN ON A DEFECT NOBODY NEEDED TO FIND, which is the opposite of gaming convergence. THE THIRD OPTION, WIDENING THE SWEEP BOUND TO WHOLE-FILE, WAS PUT AND DECLINED for the triager's own reason: it turns every remedy into an authoring pass over a long document, and authored prose is the remedy class this project has six measurements against.

`Q-55-helpsurface` DECIDED (2026-08-08, receipt record 295): FIX IT AND NOTE THE SURFACE CHANGE, over fixing it with a CHANGELOG entry and over routing it to the backlog step. THE TRANSFERABLE LESSON OF INC3, recorded because it is the calibration this whole task exists to produce: FOUR ROUNDS AND TWENTY-TWO REVIEWER PASSES ATTACKED BEHAVIOUR AND READ PROSE, AND A FIFTH ROUND FOUND WHAT THEY COULD NOT BY CHANGING THE QUESTION. A mutation-and-coverage lens asked not "is this code wrong" but "IF THIS CODE WERE WRONG, WOULD THE SUITE SAY SO", ran 30 mutations, and found 11 uncaught, one of them being that the suite could not detect the silent reversal of a human decision on any machine where the process is not stopped by a file mode. NO BEHAVIOURAL ATTACK COULD HAVE FOUND IT, because the code was correct; no reading lens could have, because the test looked right and was named correctly. RUN A MUTATION LENS ON ANY INCREMENT WHOSE CORRECTNESS RESTS ON A DECIDED CHOICE.

NOTHING ELSE IS AUTHORISED, and in particular NO SOURCE CHANGE IS AUTHORISED, since the code has been unchanged and correct since round 2 and five rounds agree on that.

THE REASONING PRESENTED, recorded because it is what makes the choice checkable rather than a preference. FOR authorising the fix: the remedy is ALREADY MEASURED green on the shipped design at uid 1000 and uid 0, red on the declined design at both, and red on both of `R5M-1`'s uncaught mutations, so there is nothing new for a further round to discover (Ground decisions in evidence); it adds NO SOURCE CHANGE, so the reviewed behaviour is untouched; and it uses a technique this repository already owns from `36e19f0`, the very commit the governing decision cites, so it introduces no new pattern (Minimal by default). AGAINST resuming the loop, which was NOT rejected on merit: it honours the two-clean-round bar rather than short-circuiting it, and this is the increment where a fix pass twice opened something new; it was declined because a round 6 on a SIX-LINE TEST-ONLY ADDITION is disproportionate to what it could find, the two previous rounds on an unchanged artifact having cost four agents apiece. AGAINST accepting the residuals: it would ship a test that CLAIMS a red-then-green in its own doc comment while demonstrating nothing on root and in containers, leaving a decided design unprotected against a later reader reintroducing the declined alternative in good faith, which is this increment's own defect class and costs six lines to avoid.

ROUND 5 RAN AND IS `new_valid` (2026-08-07, record 282): TWO valid findings, ONE `medium` and ONE `low`, from TWO raw. Streak resets to 0. THE ROUND REACHES THE CAP OF 5, AND BECAUSE IT IS NOT CLEAN THE CONVERGENCE CHECK DOES NOT PRE-EMPT IT, SO THE LOOP ESCALATES TO THE HUMAN. The artifact was UNCHANGED since round 4, re-reviewed by two FRESH lenses on mixed models because one clean round is weak evidence when reviewers are sampled stochastically. The adversarial lens, deliberately run on a DIFFERENT MODEL from every previous adversarial pass on this increment, found NOTHING; the finding came from a lens nobody had run.

THE MUTATION-AND-COVERAGE LENS ASKED THE INVERSE QUESTION FOUR ROUNDS OF ATTACK NEVER ASKED: not "is this code wrong" but "IF THIS CODE WERE WRONG, WOULD THE SUITE SAY SO". Thirty mutations, NINETEEN CAUGHT, ELEVEN NOT. That single change of question found what 22 reviewer-passes of behavioural attack and prose reading had not, and it is the most transferable lesson this step has produced.

`R5M-2` (RAISED FROM `low` TO `medium` BY THE TRIAGER) IS THAT THE SUITE CANNOT DETECT THE SILENT REVERSAL OF A HUMAN DECISION. The whole `Err` branch IS the design `Q-55-existsgate` chose over the `exists()` it declined, and its assertions live inside one `if opaque` block, so where mode 600 does not stop the process the guard never runs. THE ORCHESTRATOR REPRODUCED THIS FIRST-HAND rather than relaying it, and got the strong form: with the source reverted to the DECLINED design and NO TEST TOUCHED AT ALL, `cargo test` is 421 passed and 1 FAILED as the ordinary user but 422 passed and 0 FAILED under `unshare -Ur`. The triager had reached the same result independently, upgrading the reviewer's `&& false` simulation to a real namespace run.

WHY THE TRIAGER CALLED IT MATERIALLY DIFFERENT FROM AN ORDINARY COVERAGE GAP, ON THREE AXES, and the reasoning is worth more than the verdict. (1) IT IS A FALSE POSITIVE REPORT, NOT AN ABSENCE: a test exists, is NAMED for the branch, CLAIMS a red-then-green in its own doc comment, and prints `ok` while demonstrating nothing. A missing test can be found by grep; this cannot. (2) IT IS THE INCREMENT'S OWN DEFECT CLASS TURNED ON ITSELF: the suite skips a check and reports success, which is exactly what this increment forbids the tool to do. (3) WHAT IS UNPROTECTED IS A DECIDED CHOICE with a recorded receipt, over an alternative this ledger itself records as "NOT rejected on merit", which is precisely what a later reader might reintroduce in good faith.

THE TRIAGER LOOKED HARD FOR THE DISMISSAL AND SAID SO. The two grounds most likely to supply one, round 1's `SC-3` precedent and the reviewer's own house-convention mitigation, BOTH POINTED THE OTHER WAY on inspection: `SC-3`'s three grounds are absent or inverted here, and its own text holds the contingency open for this case. THE HOUSE-CONVENTION MITIGATION INVERTED INTO AN AGGRAVATOR, which is what moved the severity. The repository ALREADY OWNS an unconditional, uid-independent technique for this exact class, a TRAILING SLASH on a regular file, used unguarded twice in `tests/unsafe_pairings_are_refused_and_omitted.rs` with a comment saying it exists "to keep the fixture from passing vacuously", and `git log -S` places those tests in `36e19f0`, THE VERY COMMIT `Q-55-existsgate` CITES AS ITS `try_exists` PRECEDENT. The increment took the CODE precedent from that commit and left the TEST precedent lying beside it.

THE REMEDY IS MEASURED AND SMALL, AND THE TRIAGER FOUND A BETTER ONE THAN THE REVIEWER. The reviewer's ENOTDIR fixture (making `docs/metrics` a regular file) works at every uid but cannot keep a real log behind the error; a TRAILING SLASH on the log path gives `ENOTDIR` at every uid AND keeps a real, valid, readable log behind it, which is the case the reviewer had said ENOTDIR could not reproduce. Measured green on the shipped design at uid 1000 and uid 0, red on the declined design at both, and red on both of `R5M-1`'s uncaught mutations. ONE SIX-LINE ADDITION TO ONE EXISTING TEST CLOSES BOTH FINDINGS AT EVERY UID, with NO SOURCE CHANGE and NO NEW FIXTURE FILE.

ROUND 4 RAN AND IS `clean` (2026-08-06, record 281): ZERO valid findings from ONE raw. STREAK 1 OF 2, round 4 of a cap of 5. A CLEAN ROUND 5 CONVERGES. Two lenses on mixed models, adversarial-construction on opus finding one `low` and cold-conformance on sonnet finding nothing, plus a separate triager because the round had a finding to adjudicate. THE ONE DISMISSAL IS `low`, BELOW THE BACKSTOP SEVERITY, SO NO INDEPENDENT RE-CHECK IS OWED.

THE ORCHESTRATOR TOLD THE TRIAGER THE CONVERGENCE ARITHMETIC BEFORE IT RULED, DELIBERATELY, AND THE TRIAGER RECORDED THAT IT WAS TOLD. The brief stated exactly what each verdict would cause: valid means the streak stays 0 and convergence before the cap becomes arithmetically impossible so the loop escalates, while invalid or residual means the round counts clean and a clean round 5 converges. It then said the arithmetic must not move the verdict by an inch, that escalation is a DESIGNED OUTCOME rather than a failure, and that a verdict bent in either direction to reach a preferred number would be the worst thing that could happen in the loop. THE REASONING FOR STATING IT RATHER THAN HIDING IT: an unstated pressure is more dangerous than a stated one, because a triager that senses the loop is near its cap without being told cannot calibrate against a pressure it has not named. The triager's own file records both that it was told and that ONE OF ITS OWN CONSTRUCTIONS RAN AGAINST THE VERDICT IT REACHED before a corrected version resolved it.

`R4A-1` WAS RULED NOT VALID, AND THE ROUTE TO THAT RULING MATTERS MORE THAN THE RULING. The finding was that a comment this increment authored claims a non-instrumented project and a mis-anchored run "are distinguishable", while the two produce byte-identical stdout, stderr and exit code. THE TRIAGER REPRODUCED IT AND THEN MADE IT STRONGER THAN THE REVIEWER HAD: its first counterexample attempt varied the SPELLING of the mis-anchoring rather than the POPULATION, which is the DIMENSIONS TRAP the brief had warned about, and it caught its own error, corrected the experiment holding spelling fixed, and established that the message-level identity is UNIVERSAL rather than confined to accepted cost (i). That kills any sub-case defence and is a sharper result than the finding claimed.

IT STILL RULED NOT VALID, ON A SECOND SITE THE REVIEWER MISSED. The same increment states the same claim in a test doc comment WITH THE OBSERVER NAMED, "so a reader can tell a non-instrumented project from a mis-anchored run". A sentence with a true and a false reading, whose author states the true reading explicitly elsewhere in the same increment, is COMPRESSED RATHER THAN FALSE. The reader-level claim is true and naming the path is what makes it true: the printed path is the lexical resolution handed to `try_exists`, resolved against the same cwd the reader ran in, so the reader learns exactly which file was probed, where the PRE-INCREMENT message named no path and told a mis-anchored operator the log was missing, which was flatly false for them. The triager also measured that BOTH remedy clauses are LIVE, one per population, so one message with the path named lets the reader select. THE PROJECT'S OWN SETTLED LINE DECIDED IT: round 3 ruled `R3B-1`'s "only" VALID because it claimed exhaustiveness, and ruled a neighbouring sentence NOT A FINDING because it "under-describes rather than mis-states". This is under-description. RECORDED AS A RESIDUAL AND NOT TO BE RE-RAISED: the tool cannot separate the two populations at any spelling, the discrimination is the READER'S, and it is weakest under accepted cost (i) where the printed cwd-relative path is spelled identically to the project-relative path the reader calls their log.

THE ZERO-FINDING REPORT WAS TREATED AS A CLAIM AND CHECKED, WHICH IS THE HALF OF A CLEAN ROUND THAT USUALLY GOES UNEXAMINED. The triager spot-checked FOUR of the cold reviewer's discrimination results rather than the two it was asked for, on its own fixtures against its own build from `git archive 9eeca42`, because the cold reviewer had BORROWED a pre-increment binary rather than building one. Checks 15 and 18 discriminate, 18 including on a genuinely instrumented project; check 20's pre-increment scaffold carries the old `once built` sentence with no log qualifier while the new one carries the qualifier; and check 17 is a CONTROL rather than a discriminator, exactly as characterised.

THE PRE-EXISTING CONTAINMENT TOCTOU IS CONFIRMED PRE-EXISTING AND IS ROUTED, and it is the most serious thing either round-4 reviewer touched. With `--source` as a FIFO widening the window between the log read and the containment check, a mid-run symlink swap yields `workflow invariants hold` AT EXIT 0 OVER A LOG OUTSIDE THE PROJECT ROOT, with both static endpoints exiting 1. That is a FALSE GREEN, the exact defect class this step exists to remove, so its attribution was established on the MECHANISM and not merely on byte-identity: the read and the guard sit in the same order before and after, the guard is unchanged context in the diff, and the one changed line in that stretch cannot affect a race whose symlink target exists at probe time. IT IS NOT THIS INCREMENT'S. It routes to the validation-constraints step beside `R2A-4` and `R3A-3`.

A CONVENTION BREACH IN A MERGED FINDINGS FILE, recorded because the orchestrator edited a reviewer's artifact. The cold-conformance reviewer shipped FOUR EM-DASHES into its findings file despite the ASCII convention. The orchestrator normalised them to commas as INTEGRATION rather than authoring, a character-level substitution changing no meaning, committed separately at `8aa3a2b`, and added the prohibition explicitly to the round 4 triage brief. Every other findings file in this step's history has been ASCII-clean on arrival.

THE ROUND 3 FIX PASS IS DONE (2026-08-06, commits `e0871bf` and `d87c408` on `impl/wet-inc3`) AND IT IS THE SMALLEST CHANGE IN THIS STEP'S HISTORY: two files, one line each, FIVE WORDS REMOVED AND ZERO AUTHORED. The word "only" left a comment whose exhaustiveness claim round 2's own `V-3` had outgrown, and "`Path::exists`, which is" left a test doc comment naming the gate the round 2 rebinding replaced. VERIFIED BY THE ORCHESTRATOR: the whole-increment shape is UNCHANGED at 8 files, 294 insertions and 40 deletions, since both edits replace one line each inside already-changed files; 422 tests pass with 0 failures BOTH as the ordinary user AND under `unshare -Ur`; clippy is silent; `render --check` is up to date; `R3A-1`'s residual clause is intact in the `Err` message; and no orchestrator-owned file was touched.

THE IMPLEMENTER ADDED A CONTROL NOTHING ASKED FOR, AND IT IS THE DISCIPLINE THIS TASK HAS SPENT THREE INCREMENTS LEARNING. Told to prove plain `validate` byte-identical to a pre-increment build on the unsearchable-ancestor fixture, it observed that an identity result is WORTHLESS IF THE TWO BINARIES MIGHT NOT DIFFER AT ALL, so it ran `--workflow` on the SAME fixture and showed they diverge there, the new build exiting 1 with `could not be checked (Permission denied)` where the old exits 0 with the skip note. The identity is therefore genuine rather than vacuous. It also confirmed its pre-increment build was really pre-increment by checking that its only `could not be checked` string is the unrelated one and that it still carries `metrics_path.exists()`.

IT ALSO DECLINED A NEARBY EDIT THAT WOULD HAVE LOOKED LIKE A SECOND INSTANCE OF THE SAME FINDING, correctly. Another comment mentions `Path::exists`, but as a DELIBERATE CONTRAST between two APIs, saying `try_exists` separates the cases `Path::exists` collapses into one `false`, which is a true statement about the API rather than a claim about which gate the code calls. Treating a matching string as a matching defect is how a currency pass over-reaches, and it did not.

ROUND 3 RAN AND IS `new_valid` (2026-08-06, record 280): TWO valid findings from FOUR raw, BOTH `low`, plus ONE accepted as a residual and ONE ruled out of scope. ZERO dismissed at or above the backstop severity, so NO backstop was owed. Streak still 0 of 2, ROUND 3 OF A CAP OF 5. TWO LENSES on mixed models (adversarial-construction on opus, closure-and-comment-truth on sonnet) plus a separate triager. THE SEVERITY CEILING FELL AGAIN, high/high/medium/high across inc2's four rounds, then medium, medium, and now `low`: the first round in this step to top out below medium. THE ENTIRE REMEDY IS TWO DELETIONS TOTALLING FIVE WORDS WITH ZERO AUTHORED, and neither touches behaviour.

THE ROUND TURNED ON A CONTROL NEITHER REVIEWER RAN, AND IT REVERSED THE ADVERSARIAL LENS'S HEADLINE FINDING. `R3A-1` reported that round 2's `V-3` clause, `; pass a --metrics naming this project's log`, is USELESS ADVICE ON EACCES, because on the mode-600 fixture the resolved path already IS this project's log, so obeying the instruction returns byte-identical stdout, stderr and exit code; it proposed deleting the nine-word clause. THE TRIAGER REPRODUCED THAT EXACTLY, then varied the dimension the reviewer had held fixed: with the project's real log readable at its conventional path AND an explicit `--metrics` naming a file under a mode-600 directory, a sub-case round 1's own triage had already recorded as reachable, obeying the SAME instruction on the SAME errno converts exit 1 into a correct `workflow invariants hold` at exit 0. SO THE CLAUSE IS LIVE ON EACCES; what is inert is EACCES UNDER THE DEFAULT ANCHOR. `R3A-1` varied errno and held ANCHORING fixed, so its fixture could not discriminate, which is THE DIMENSIONS FAILURE, the first of the three shapes that defeated the previous increment's review, recurring here and caught this time by the triager rather than a round later.

THE CONSEQUENCE FOR THE TWO CONFLICTING REMEDIES, RESOLVED AS ONE DECISION RATHER THAN TWO. `R3A-1`'s deletion is FEWER WORDS but removes FOUR TRUE, LIVE CLAIMS (ENOTDIR, ELOOP, ENAMETOOLONG, and a correctly-named EACCES) to remove ONE INERT, NON-FALSE hint, and would regress `V-3`. `R3B-1`'s one-word deletion of "only" removes EXACTLY the one false claim and nothing true, and is SAFE UNDER BOTH FUTURES: if a human later overrules the residual and deletes the clause after all, the comment still reads true, so it never has to be undone. `R3A-1` IS THEREFORE ACCEPTED AS A RESIDUAL AND MUST NOT BE RE-RAISED. `V-3`'S VERDICT AND SHIPPED REMEDY STAND UNCHANGED; what was over-broad was a SUPPORTING SENTENCE in round 2's triage calling the clause "also useful advice" on EACCES, and `R3A-1`'s claim to have measured that false is over-broad in the mirror direction.

`R3A-3` IS OUT OF SCOPE FOR THIS INCREMENT AND IS ROUTED, on three independent grounds the triager gave rather than one. It reports that the check's PLAN-SOURCE input makes `T-1`'s exact false claim under `T-1`'s exact fixture shape, a mode-600 `docs/plans` yielding "no source plan at X" with a remedy aimed at someone who already passed one. It is BYTE-IDENTICAL on all three builds, so pre-existing, not introduced; the line it names is CONTEXT in the diff, not a changed line; the ledger's own locked scope for this increment names only the `_` catch-all plus the docs and the test; and its remedy sits on a gate whose `else` branch feeds plain `validate`, which is precisely the change `Q-55-existsgate` DECLINED. IT GOES TO THE VALIDATION-CONSTRAINTS STEP, beside `R2A-4` and the queued plain-`validate` inconsistency. Same family, NOT the same defect: that one is about the LOG input's exit codes, this one about the PLAN-SOURCE input's message.

THE CLOSURE TABLE WAS CHECKED RATHER THAN TAKEN, AND IT HOLDS AT TEN OF TEN. The closure lens reproduced all ten prior findings against the tip with their original fixtures; the triager then re-checked ALL TEN rather than the three it was asked for. `T-1` closed on both mode-600 and mode-000 with the pre-increment build giving the old exit-0 skip and the 755 control reading the log clean. `V-2`'s cell 2 was REBUILT FROM SCRATCH with a FIFO widener: the current build prints the true sentence where the pre-fix build printed the false `could not be checked`. `T-3` closed WITH ITS DISCRIMINATING CONTROL, the old hyphenated spelling still failing. Gates re-measured at 422/0, clippy clean, `unshare -Ur` clean, and plain `validate` byte-identical to the pre-increment build across 15 inputs.

THREE THINGS THE REVIEWERS MISSED, none raised as findings and each recorded because the reasoning is the useful part. FIRST, `ce820fb`, the branch's newest commit, IS GUARDED BY NO TEST: the triager applied `R3A-1`'s deletion and the suite still passed 422/0, so the entire commit can be deleted silently. NOT raised, on round 1's own `SC-3` precedent, since the untested thing is nine words of advice hanging off an error path whose behaviour IS asserted; it becomes a coherent proposal once the human settles the residual. SECOND, the `run_validate` doc comment is one case short of the clap help beside it, and the triager says explicitly it should NOT be fixed, because it under-describes rather than mis-states and every remedy adds words. THIRD, NOTHING SCAFFOLDED RUNS `validate --workflow`: only prose in `pack/instrument.md` mentions it and the scaffolded pre-commit hook does not invoke it, so the exit-code flip breaks no scaffolded project's gate. That is a blast-radius negative neither reviewer checked and it is the strongest single piece of evidence for `Safe on existing projects` that this increment has.

THE ROUND 2 FIX PASS IS DONE (2026-08-06, commits `d88d576`, `ede3a7b`, `331c9d9` and `dc1c70b` on `impl/wet-inc3`), AND IT IS THE SMALLEST PASS IN THIS STEP'S HISTORY: 2 files, 16 insertions and 15 deletions, SEVENTEEN words authored against NINE deleted, of which the seven user-facing ones are `V-3`'s clause COPIED VERBATIM from the neighbouring string. No new sentence exists anywhere in it. Round 1's comparable pass authored 498. THE COMMITS WERE ORDERED SO EVERY ONE IS GREEN IN BOTH ENVIRONMENTS FROM THE FIRST ONWARD, with the root-fragility fix first, which is a discipline nothing asked for.

GATES RE-RUN BY THE ORCHESTRATOR IN BOTH ENVIRONMENTS, not relayed: 422 tests passing with 0 failures AS THE ORDINARY USER and 422 with 0 failures UNDER `unshare -Ur` AS NAMESPACE ROOT, clippy silent at 0 matching lines inside the flake toolchain, `render --check` up to date, and ZERO orchestrator-owned files touched. THE ROOT RUN IS THE ONE THAT MATTERS THIS ROUND: before this pass the same suite was 421 passed and 1 FAILED as root, and the failure was the test the round 1 fix pass had added.

`T-1`'S CLOSURE SURVIVED THE `V-2` REBINDING, WHICH THE ORCHESTRATOR CHECKED RATHER THAN ASSUMED, because a rebinding of the very probe `T-1`'s fix introduced is exactly where a silent regression would sit. On the static mode-600 fixture the tool still prints `could not be checked (Permission denied (os error 13))` at exit 1, now carrying `V-3`'s copied remedy clause, and plain `validate` on that same fixture is still BYTE-IDENTICAL to a build from `9eeca42` predating the whole increment. The implementer independently widened its own check-16 verification far past the brief, to 39 comparisons across 13 inputs and three directory modes against TWO baseline binaries, all byte-identical.

A NEW ORCHESTRATOR DEFECT, (16), CAUGHT BY THE IMPLEMENTER'S ESCAPE VALVE, AND IT IS THE SAME CLASS AS DEFECT (15). THE DEFECT: the closing brief told the implementer to "correct the comment that claims `Ok` asserts absence, which is false about a live branch". That clause is the TRIAGE'S OBSERVATION ABOUT THE PRE-FIX CODE, where `Ok(true)` was reachable in the arm; the `V-2` rebinding the same brief commissioned makes `Ok(true)` UNREACHABLE BY CONSTRUCTION, so after the fix the comment becomes TRUE. FOLLOWING THE BRIEF LITERALLY WOULD HAVE DELETED A NOW-TRUE STATEMENT AND LOST INFORMATION. The implementer refused it, said so, and corrected instead the two claims the rebinding ACTUALLY made stale, the naming of `Path::exists` and the description of what the gate keeps, adding that the SAME probe splits the cases so the single-probe fact is on the record. THE ROOT CAUSE IS IDENTICAL TO (15): a triage statement measured against the PRE-FIX build was transcribed into a POST-FIX instruction. THE STANDING CURE: before quoting a triage observation into a fix brief, establish whether the commissioned fix changes its truth value, because a triage describes the code as it stands and a brief instructs on the code as it will be.

THE IMPLEMENTER ALSO FLAGGED, RATHER THAN BURIED, THREE THINGS ROUND 3 SHOULD CHECK. It wrote an explanatory comment at the gate and then REMOVED it to honour the no-new-sentence constraint, leaving the gate line reading `try_exists()` with no local note, and named the residual risk itself: a reader at that line could think `Q-55-existsgate`'s DECLINED change had been made, with only a comment 210 lines below and the commit message saying otherwise. `V-3`'s clause now also lands on EACCES, so the static mode-600 sentence is no longer byte-identical to what the round 1 fix produced; the triage endorsed this explicitly but it is a change to the sentence `T-1` created. And it did not reflow a now-ragged comment, correctly, since this project forbids reflowing prose to a width.

TWO SMALL DEFECTS IN THE ROUND 2 TRIAGE ITSELF, recorded for calibration rather than action: it called `V-3`'s clause "nine words" where it is seven, and it labelled `V-4`'s remedy "A PURE DELETION" where it is a substitution with glue at four words authored against six deleted. Its arithmetic was right and only the label overstated. This is the second round running in which a findings artifact carried a small counting or citation error while the substance held.

ROUND 2 RAN AND IS `new_valid` (2026-08-06, record 279): FOUR valid findings from SEVEN raw, ONE `medium` and THREE `low`, plus ONE accepted as a residual and TWO ruled INVALID. ZERO dismissed at or above the backstop severity, so NO backstop was owed. Streak still 0 of 2, round 2 of a cap of 5. TWO LENSES on mixed models (adversarial-construction on opus, fix-induced-residue on sonnet) plus a separate triager, all three from the branch tip. THE TWO LENSES OVERLAPPED ON NOTHING, so seven raw findings deduplicated to seven unique, which is the opposite of round 1 where three of eleven were found twice. EVERY REMEDY THIS ROUND IS A DELETION, A MOVE, A REBINDING OR A CLAUSE COPIED FROM A NEIGHBOURING STRING: NO NEW SENTENCE IS AUTHORED ANYWHERE IN THE SET, which by this project's own measured rule is the shape that does not re-seed the next round.

THE QUESTION THIS ROUND EXISTED TO SETTLE WAS WHETHER A ROUND 1 FIX RE-OPENED THE DEFECT IT CLOSED, AND THE ANSWER IS NO, ESTABLISHED BY CONSTRUCTION RATHER THAN BY ARGUMENT. `R2A-2` reported that the gate which stats the log and the arm which stats it again are two observations, and that one disagreement direction prints `T-1`'s EXACT false sentence over a log that is present with a real record, through the branch `T-1`'s fix added. The triager built both race cells deterministically with a FIFO and found the framing WRONG IN A WAY THAT MATTERS: that direction is INHERITED, NOT OPENED, because the pre-increment build prints the IDENTICAL sentence on the IDENTICAL fixture, the gate's observation having been TRUE WHEN IT WAS MADE, and no single-stat implementation can change that cell. `T-1` itself is closed on every static filesystem. WHAT THE FIX DID INTRODUCE is the OPPOSITE direction, a false `could not be checked` where the tool had successfully answered and the old build printed the true sentence, plus a comment that is false about a live branch. Upheld at `low` and NOT raised: the exit code is 1 and correct in every cell, there is no false green, and both cells require a MID-RUN filesystem change where `T-1` was static and fired every time. THIS IS THE FIRST TIME IN THIS STEP THAT A SUSPECTED FIX-INDUCED REOPENING WAS MEASURED AND FOUND NOT TO BE ONE, and the discriminating control is the one the reviewer did not run: the same fixture against the pre-increment binary.

`R2A-1` (medium) IS A GENUINE PORTABILITY DEFECT IN THE TEST THE FIX PASS ADDED, reproduced by the triager rather than accepted: an `assert_eq!(code, Some(1))` sits OUTSIDE the `if opaque` guard, so in an environment where mode 600 does not hide directory entries the check runs, succeeds, exits 0, and the test fails on an assertion whose message claims the check could not run. Under `unshare -Ur` as namespace root, HEAD is 421 passed and 1 failed and the failure is exactly that test, while main is 420/0 and the pre-fix tip is 421/0, so the attribution is clean and NO OTHER TEST IN THE REPOSITORY IS ROOT-FRAGILE. The remedy is one assertion moved inside the existing guard, measured at 422/0 both as the user and as root.

`R2B-3` WAS A FINDING AGAINST THE ORCHESTRATOR'S OWN LEDGER TEXT, AND IT ESTABLISHED A STANDING RULE THAT OUTLIVES IT. The finding claimed the fix-pass sentence "422 tests passing ... up from 421 by the one new test" was wrong at a true baseline of 420 with two tests added. RULED INVALID, MIS-SCOPED: the triager measured main at 420, the build tip at 421 and HEAD at 422, with 2, 3 and 4 test functions, so both ledger sentences are true against their own correctly named baselines, one naming main's 420 and the other naming the pre-fix-pass tip's 421. THE REVIEWER'S ERROR WAS A RANGE, NOT A COUNT: it targeted `18176fa..HEAD` as "the fix pass alone", but the rebase had put the code commits ON TOP OF the round 1 docs, so that range is the whole increment and it charged the build's test to the fix pass. THE ORCHESTRATOR HAD REACHED THE SAME CONCLUSION AND DELIBERATELY DID NOT RULE, passing its measurement to the triager with an explicit instruction not to accept the reasoning because the orchestrator offered it; the triager measured first and read the reasoning after. THE STANDING RULE: the ledger is NOT in `git diff main...HEAD` and is not the reviewed product, so a defect in it is REPORTED TO THE ORCHESTRATOR AS A CORRECTION TO THE DURABLE RECORD AND NEVER COUNTED IN A ROUND'S TALLY, because the tally drives convergence and the ledger is the orchestrator's own artifact. Counting it would let the orchestrator's bookkeeping reset a streak.

`R2B-2` WAS RULED INVALID ON THE SPECIFICATION, WHICH NEITHER REVIEWER HAD OPENED. It proposed DELETING the `--instrument` clause from the tier sentence as residue the `T-2` fix left. The step's own documentation-impact block REQUIRES that clause in `pack/AGENTS.md` and in the CHANGELOG and names the "when instrumentation is on" phrasing as the house form to match, so the deletion would breach the spec; independently, the triager reproduced that the sentence's operative rule predicts check 15's exit code correctly on BOTH populations. THE LESSON IS ABOUT REMEDIES, NOT CLAIMS: a reviewer formed a remedy against three sentences the specification governs without reading what the specification required of them.

`R2A-4` WAS ACCEPTED AS A RESIDUAL AND MUST NOT BE RE-RAISED: the pre-existing `no metrics log at <path>` note still prints one line above the corrected sentence. Reproduced, but its prose half is either `Q-55-existsgate`'s DECLINED change or a fresh decision, and the test assertion it proposed would fail today. Routed into the queued validation-constraints item alongside the pre-existing plain-`validate` inconsistency.

THE CLIPPY DISAGREEMENT WAS AN ENVIRONMENT DISAGREEMENT, WHICH IS THIS PROJECT'S OWN RECORDED FAILURE MODE FIRING AGAIN. The residue lens reported a `-D warnings` `dead_code` failure the orchestrator did not see. The triager established the cause: `/usr/bin/cargo` 1.88 sits ahead of the nix store on the default PATH, that failure reproduces there AND at `main`, and the flake toolchain's clippy 0.1.98 is clean at HEAD. The project's gate is clean and that reviewer's build-dependent claims were made OUTSIDE the project environment. The orchestrator suspected this from the disagreement alone and put it in the triage brief as something to establish before ruling, rather than dismissing the claim or accepting it.

`Q-55-existsgate` DECIDED (2026-08-06, receipt): FIX `T-1` WITH AN ARM-SCOPED MESSAGE SPLIT, distinguishing absent from cannot-tell inside the new `--workflow` arm only, over the one-token `exists()` to `try_exists()?` change and over accepting `T-1` as a residual. AGAINST accepting the residual: the same unreleased CHANGELOG promises the user this exact distinction ONE BULLET BELOW the new entry, so the release would tell users the tool separates these cases while the mechanism it ships does not separate them for the log.

Streak 0 of 2, round 1 of a cap of 5. THREE LENSES on mixed models (adversarial-construction and documentation-truth on opus, scope-and-coverage on sonnet) plus a separate triager on a fourth worktree, all four created from the branch tip. THE SEVERITY CEILING IS `medium`, WHICH IS THE LOWEST ANY ROUND 1 HAS OPENED AT IN THIS STEP: inc1 and inc2 both opened at `high`.

THREE OF THE ELEVEN WERE FOUND TWICE BY DIFFERENT LENSES REACHING THEM BY DIFFERENT ROUTES, which is corroboration rather than duplication and is recorded because this task has never before had three independent confirmations in one round. `T-2` was reached by the documentation lens through the prose and by the adversarial lens through the code. `T-5` was reached by the documentation lens and by the scope lens. `T-3` was found by the adversarial lens, which identified the CAUSE, and independently by the scope lens, which traced it to the schema's own status list.

`T-1` (medium, from `ADV-1`) IS THE ROUND'S SUBSTANCE AND IT IS THE SAME FAMILY AS THE LAST INCREMENT'S WORST DEFECT, REACHED THROUGH A NEW DOOR. The new problem is gated on `Path::exists()`, which answers false BOTH for "the log is absent" AND for "the existence question could not be answered", so the tool asserts absence about a path where a real log sits and tells the operator to record rounds that are already recorded there. THE APPARENT CONTRADICTION IN THE REVIEWER'S OWN REPORT WAS RECONCILED BY THE TRIAGER RATHER THAN LEFT STANDING, which is what the round turned on: the same reviewer reported under its negative results that a mode-000 log file and a directory at the log path both propagate the io error instead. Both statements are true because the two calls split the space. `exists()` is `metadata().is_ok()` and the read inside the branch is `read_to_string(..)?`; where `metadata` SUCCEEDS every failure belongs to the read and propagates, and where `metadata` FAILS `exists()` answers false and the new arm asserts absence. Disjoint sets. THE TRIAGER THEN CONSTRUCTED EVERY ARRANGEMENT IN THE METADATA-FAILS SET (ELOOP at the path and in an ancestor, ENOTDIR, dangling symlink, ENAMETOOLONG, EACCES) and found EXACTLY ONE with a real log behind it: AN ANCESTOR DIRECTORY OF THE RESOLVED METRICS PATH THAT DENIES SEARCH PERMISSION, bounded to at or below the level that does not also block the plan source, since with `docs` itself unsearchable the run correctly reports no plan source resolved instead. A NON-EMPTY REACHABLE SET NAMED PRECISELY IS WHAT MAKES THIS A FINDING RATHER THAN A CLASS ARGUMENT, and an empty one would have made it invalid however good the reasoning.

THE ORCHESTRATOR REPRODUCED `T-1` FIRST-HAND ON THE TRIAGER'S SHARPER FIXTURE, mode 600 rather than 000, which the original reviewer missed and which is the version that makes the falsehood undeniable: `ls docs/metrics` prints `workflow.jsonl` for the same user in the same shell while the tool prints `--workflow requested but no round log at docs/metrics/workflow.jsonl` and advises recording rounds there, at exit 1. The control at mode 755 reads the log and exits 0.

WHAT MAKES `T-1` MORE THAN A COSMETIC DIAGNOSIS, and the triager found it while reproducing rather than being asked: THE SAME UNRELEASED CHANGELOG ALREADY PROMISES THE USER THIS EXACT DISTINCTION ONE BULLET BELOW THE NEW ONE, for a directory above an anchor that the process cannot traverse, saying the note reports that the check FAILED rather than that the path is MISSING. That bullet is scoped to anchors so it is not itself false, but it means one release tells users the tool separates these two cases for `--source` and `--plan` while the mechanism this increment adds does not separate them for the log.

THE SAFETY ARGUMENT, MEASURED AND NOT ASSUMED, because 46 of the 48 were NOT ancestors of main and a patch-id check flagged 30 of them as carrying unique commits: for every commit that `git cherry` reported as unique, every file it touched was checked against main's history for a blob of IDENTICAL CONTENT AT THAT PATH, and the only 12 misses were all intermediate snapshots of the append-only `docs/metrics/workflow.jsonl`, each of which was then diffed record-by-record against main's current log and found to contain ZERO records main lacks.
