THE IMMEDIATE NEXT ACTION IS TO PLAN AND BUILD `workflow-enforcement-tier-inc2`, AND A HUMAN DECISION IS OWED BEFORE IT IS BUILT. THE DECISION, raised by inc1's round 2 review and deliberately left unpicked by the triager because the choice is the human's: THE STEP'S END PROPERTY IS CURRENTLY MET BY NO INCREMENT AS SPECIFIED. When a Markdown-primary `--source` names one project and `--plan` names another, the away plan is checked against the home project's log and reports `workflow invariants hold` at exit 0. Inc1's code is spec-conformant; INC2 AS WRITTEN DOES NOT CLOSE IT, because the resolved log sits UNDER the anchor's root so a predicate rooted on the anchor never fires; and inc3 does not fire either because the log is present. This is recorded in the sidecar at the paragraph beginning "MEASURED AT WORK REVIEW, AND NOT CLOSED BY THIS PREDICATE AS WRITTEN". THE TWO CANDIDATES, recorded verbatim and neither chosen: root the predicate on THE PLAN THE CHECK ACTUALLY READS, or add a SECOND CONDITION on the anchor and the checked plan resolving to different roots. PUT THIS TO THE HUMAN BEFORE INC2 IS PLANNED, with trade-offs, per the human-input contract. NOTE THE DEFECT IS PRE-EXISTING, NOT A REGRESSION: the round 2 triager measured that the pre-increment binary produces the same false green byte-for-byte at exit 0.

THE DECISION IS TAKEN (2026-08-02, receipt `q_id:"Q-55-endproperty"`), SO THE PARAGRAPH ABOVE IS DISCHARGED AND THE NEXT ACTION IS A PLANNER FOLD. CHOSEN: ROOT THE PREDICATE ON THE PLAN THE CHECK ACTUALLY READS, over layering a second condition on the two roots, and over parsing the whole `(source, plan, metrics)` triple at the boundary into one validated checked-target type. THE ORCHESTRATOR VERIFIED THE MECHANISM IN THE CODE FIRST-HAND rather than relaying the sidecar: `run_validate` resolves the log through `resolve_metrics_path`, which anchors on `--source` first and `--plan` second, while the `--workflow` match arms read a DIFFERENT artifact, the TOML source in the `(Some(source), _, Some(metrics_text))` arm and `--plan` in the `(None, Some(plan_text), Some(metrics_text))` arm. THE REASONING PRESENTED, recorded because it is what makes the choice checkable rather than a preference. (1) It is ONE predicate, not two layered ones, collapsing the whole question to "is the log I am about to read under the root of the plan I am about to check" (Minimal by default). (2) It is a STRICT SUPERSET of the converged anchor-rooted text rather than a replacement: in TOML-primary mode the checked plan IS the anchor, so the rule is unchanged there, and it differs from the converged text ONLY when a `--source` is given and is not TOML-primary, which is exactly the gap. (3) It COVERS THE TYPO'D-`--source` CASE, which the second-condition option does not, because a nonexistent `--source` yields no canonical root and so a two-root comparison has nothing to compare, which is the same no-root clause the sidecar already relies on. (4) The boundary-parse option was rejected on scope, not on merit: it is the cleaner long-term architecture and would make the divergent pairing unconstructible rather than detected (Make illegal states unrepresentable, Prefer the cleaner long-term architecture over the smallest diff), but it touches the resolution shared by `validate`, `status`, `next` and the ledger path, needs its own risk class and probably its own design pass, and risks reopening the already-decided `Q-55-refusalscope` split between the validator refusing and the projections omitting. IF IT IS EVER WANTED it belongs in the validation-constraints step alongside the queued project-identity work, which is the other mechanism that reaches the same class.

Round-record task id `workflow-enforcement-tier-endproperty-fold` with the structured `step` field set, a NEW id rather than a reuse of `workflow-enforcement-tier-fold`, because this is a fresh loop and reusing the id would inherit a converged loop's counters; the structured `step` field means the W3 join reads it directly and NO `[meta].orphan_tasks` entry is owed, following the `decision-folder-currency-fold` precedent.

ROUND 1 LENSES, chosen from this task's own recorded lessons rather than by habit: EXECUTABILITY and FIDELITY-plus-INTERNAL-CONSISTENCY, run on MIXED MODELS (opus and sonnet) per the diversity rule this task violated through round 2 of an earlier loop and has honoured since. The executability lens runs at ROUND 1 DELIBERATELY: the plan fold's round-4 finding `H4-1` was the single most important finding of that whole loop and arrived only when a reviewer read the sidecar as INSTRUCTIONS TO FOLLOW rather than as a document, after four prior rounds had read it as a document and passed it, and the lesson recorded at the time was to run an executability lens EARLY. Both lenses are scoped to a REGION rather than to a list of sentences, which is the round-3 correction from step 93's loop where a zero-finding lens missed two defects inside its own remit but outside its sweep.

THE ONE JUDGEMENT THE PLANNER ESCALATED, AND THE HUMAN'S RULING (2026-08-02). The planner extended the decided rooting to `next`'s DEFAULT LEDGER PATH, a consequence it derived from the code rather than from the receipt's text, and it flagged this in its own report as the one place to push back rather than letting it pass silently, which is the escape valve working as designed. THE ORCHESTRATOR VERIFIED THE DERIVATION FIRST-HAND before putting it to the human: `src/main.rs:default_ledger_path` anchors `source.as_ref().or(plan.as_ref())`, source first, while `run_next` selects its plan TOML-primary-`--source`-else-`--plan`, so a Markdown-primary `--source` in project A paired with a `--plan` in project B makes `next` project B's steps while echoing A's `## RESUME STATE`. That is CONTENT INJECTION INTO AN INSTRUCTION AN AGENT ACTS ON, which this task's design pass already recorded explorer B reproducing, and which the ledger already called arguably the more urgent half. THE HUMAN CHOSE TO KEEP IT IN INC2, over cutting back to `validate` only and filing the ledger case separately, and over building it as its own increment. The reasoning presented: the sidecar's own rule is one predicate never re-implemented per surface, so leaving the ledger rooted on the anchor while the plan root moved would have made the document contradict itself; and splitting it into a separate increment would contradict this step's settled argument, recorded when `Q-55-scope` was decided, that the four call sites share one function and splitting would review the same derivation twice (Minimal by default).

ROUND 1 RAN AND IS `new_valid` (2026-08-02, record 251): SIX valid findings, THREE of them `high`, ZERO dismissed, so NO backstop was owed. Streak 0 of 2, round 1 of a cap of 5. Two lenses on mixed models plus a separate triager on a third worktree, all three from the branch tip. THE EXECUTABILITY LENS AT ROUND 1 PAID FOR ITSELF IMMEDIATELY, which is the whole reason it was moved this early: `EX-1` (high) is that NO ACCEPTANCE CHECK PINS THE DIVERGENT-PAIRING METRICS CASE ON A PROJECTION. Every projection check uses an EXPLICIT `--metrics` outside the anchor's root, which the OLD anchor-rooted predicate catches too, so an implementer could root the ledger predicate on the checked plan, leave the metrics predicate on the anchor, PASS THE ENTIRE CHECK SET, and still have `next` print `state: converged` and `next: mark the step complete` for a foreign project. The reviewer reproduced the fabricated instruction and the RESUME STATE echo for real at exit 0. A check that passes before the change is worthless, and five of this file's projection checks were that.

THE ORCHESTRATOR'S OWN REASONING WAS FALSIFIED BY `EX-3`, AND THE CORRECTION IS RECORDED HERE RATHER THAN QUIETLY DROPPED. Reasoning point (2) in the paragraph above, that the new rooting is a STRICT SUPERSET of the converged anchor-rooted text differing ONLY on the gap case, IS FALSE, and it was presented to the human as a ground for choosing this mechanism. The triager constructed both directions and MEASURED them: the refusal sets are INCOMPARABLE, NOT NESTED. Direction 1, `--source fixC/docs/plans/x.plan.toml --plan fixC/notes/p.md` greens today and the checked plan's root resolves to `.../fixC/notes` through `src/main.rs:project_root_of_source`'s fallback to the plan's own parent when there is no `docs/plans`-shaped ancestor, so THE NEW RULE REFUSES A CORRECT SAME-PROJECT RUN. Direction 2, `--source fixA/... --plan fixB/... --metrics fixB/own-log` is B-against-B and the new rule ALLOWS it where an anchor rooting refuses it. The clause "which is exactly the gap" is false too: the gap is one of at least THREE distinguishable cases in that region. WHAT SURVIVES is only the narrower region claim, that the two rootings coincide in TOML-primary mode and where no `--source` is given. Grounds (1) and (3) are unaffected and the triager reproduced the typo'd-`--source` case that carries (3). A RE-DECISION WAS NOT INDICATED and was not taken: the REJECTED second-condition alternative does not avoid direction 1 either, because for that fixture the anchor root `.../fixC` and the checked root `.../fixC/notes` differ so a two-root condition fires on the same run, and the layout is ALREADY refused today in its no-`--source` spelling, so the amendment removes a rescue rather than introducing a new species.

TWO FURTHER HUMAN DECISIONS CLOSED ROUND 1'S OPEN QUESTIONS (2026-08-02), both put with the correction above stated first. `Q-55-conventionlesscost` (receipt): ACCEPT DIRECTION 1 AND RECORD IT AS A THIRD ACCEPTED COST beside the existing (i) silent-miss-inside-the-right-project and (ii) symlinked-`docs/plans` costs, and PIN IT WITH A CHECK, over carving the conventionless case out of the predicate and over reopening the boundary-parse option. Carving it out was declined because it would REVERSE `Q-55-noconvention`, which decided the containment refusal is layered ON TOP of the source's-own-directory fallback rather than suppressed by it. `Q-55-resumepairing` (receipt): CLOSE THE `status --resume` LEAK IN INC2, over filing it to the validation-constraints step and over reopening the boundary parse. THE DEFECT, which the triager caught as the MIRROR of a question already asked and answered for `next` but never asked for this surface: `status --resume --source A --plan B` prints project A's `## RESUME STATE` on the DEFAULT ledger path at exit 0 and SURVIVES INC2 AS SPECIFIED, because that surface reads no plan and so keeps the anchor rooting by design, while the sidecar separately asserts the default ledger case "is already closed by the anchoring in inc1". THE FRAMING THAT KEEPS THE ONE-PREDICATE RULE INTACT, and the reason this is not a per-surface re-implementation: the rule stays "root on the plan the check reads", and where a surface reads NO plan the two NAMED plans must agree or the block is omitted, which SUPPLIES a root where there is no checked plan rather than inventing a second rule.

`R2A-2` (high) IS THE SAME DEFECT SPECIES AS ROUND 1's `EX-2`, REAPPEARING INSIDE THE FIX FOR `EX-3`: accepted cost (iii) and its new pinning check 19b both omit the load-bearing precondition that the `--source` must be MARKDOWN-PRIMARY. CORRECTION, APPENDED RATHER THAN REWRITTEN PER THIS TASK'S OWN CONVENTION: THE BOUND RECORDED ABOVE FOR `Q-55-conventionlesscost` IS FALSE AS WRITTEN. THE LESSON, which is the third instance of one species in this task: a bound MEASURED with a qualifier loses the qualifier when it is transcribed into a summary, exactly as the aggregate-restating-a-detail defects the inc1 loop kept finding.

`Q-55-resumecost` DECIDED (2026-08-02, receipt): ACCEPT THE `status --resume` COST AS ACCEPTED COST (iv) AND PIN IT, AND QUEUE THE SHARED ROOT CAUSE to the validation-constraints step as ONE treatment, over accepting it with nothing queued and over carving the conventionless case out. THE INTERACTION THAT DECIDED IT: after `R2A-2`'s narrowing, cost (iii)'s metrics half is `primary`-CONDITIONAL while the resume rule is NOT, so this cost bites a WIDER POPULATION than the cost already accepted. THE QUEUED ITEM: costs (iii) and (iv) share ONE root cause, `src/main.rs:project_root_of_source`'s fallback to the plan's own parent where there is no `docs/plans`-shaped ancestor, and treating it ONCE in the validation-constraints step is better than accumulating a fresh accepted cost on every new surface (Prefer the cleaner long-term architecture over the smallest diff, One source of truth). A NEW ORCHESTRATOR DEFECT, (12), CAUSED BY THE ORCHESTRATOR AND CAUGHT BY THE PLANNER'S ESCAPE VALVE. THE DEFECT: the orchestrator appended the `Q-55-resumecost` receipt to `docs/metrics/workflow.jsonl` DURING an open review loop, and the artifact under review ASSERTED A COUNT OF THOSE RECEIPTS ("NINE decision receipts"), so an orchestrator integration edit SILENTLY FALSIFIED A CLAIM IN THE ARTIFACT ITS OWN REVIEWERS HAD JUST VERIFIED AS TRUE. The round-2 triage had checked NINE and found it correct; the tenth receipt landed after. NO REVIEWER OR TRIAGER COULD HAVE CAUGHT THIS, because the falsification happened after their reads, which is what makes it a distinct defect rather than a missed finding. THE PLANNER FOUND IT during the fix pass, RAISED IT WITHOUT FIXING IT because no finding named that line, and NAMED THE MINIMAL REMEDY ITSELF. THE CURE, APPLIED: the orchestrator sent the producer back for a SINGLE AUTHORISED ONE-WORD DELETION, removing the asserted count entirely rather than updating it to TEN, so it cannot go stale again; this is the same deletion-class remedy the triage prescribed for `R2A-7` and for `R2A-4`'s "TWO CASES REACH IT", and the planner confirmed zero words authored and no other count moved by a full uppercase-number-word census. THE STANDING RULE THIS ESTABLISHES: an artifact under review MUST NOT assert a count of anything the orchestrator appends to during the loop (receipts, round records, escalations), because the orchestrator's own integration edits are then a source of findings that no review pass can prevent. Prefer no count at all over a maintained one.

ROUND 3 IS OPEN with two lenses that have NOT run on this artifact, both from the new branch tip, models MIXED and swapped again so no lens repeats a model-and-role pairing: FIX-VERIFICATION-plus-RESIDUE (sonnet) targeted at the 77 authored words and at what the deletions may have left dangling, and THE COLD READER (opus), briefed to read the whole document FRONT TO BACK WITHOUT starting from the diffs or the prior findings, and only afterwards to consult them. THE COLD READER IS THE LENS THIS ARTIFACT HAS NOT HAD: all four previous lenses read it as PARTICIPANTS who knew the history, and on a sibling artifact in this project a cold-reader lens found two genuine prose defects that four prior rounds had walked past, at sites no fix had touched. THE COLD READER EARNED ITS PLACE BY ANSWERING ITS OWN QUESTION IN THE NEGATIVE, WHICH IS THE OUTCOME THAT MAKES A CLEAN ROUND MEAN SOMETHING. `R3B-1` WAS DISMISSED, AND THE DISMISSAL IS MORE INSTRUCTIVE THAN THE FINDING. The cold reader claimed the acceptance-check set never re-runs the no-regression correct case after inc2, so nothing pins the lexical/canonical split the document says MUST NOT BE COLLAPSED. The triager verified all three legs: legs 1 and 2 REPRODUCED (check 9 is labelled AFTER INC1, and nothing in the inc2 block re-runs it), but LEG 3 IS FALSE. The same test file the reviewer cited contains `the_correct_case_prints_the_same_relative_paths_it_always_did`, whose doc comment opens "Acceptance check 9, the Safe on existing projects pin", runs a BARE RELATIVE `--source` from the project root via `.current_dir(dir)`, and `assert_eq!`s the WHOLE stdout against the three relative spellings inline. The triager measured that the printed paths are the resolved paths verbatim, so a collapsed split FAILS that assertion, making the suite pin STRICTER than the check the reviewer said was missing. The reviewer had read the cross-project half of the file, which the module doc itself describes as content-asserted, and generalised to the whole file. Dismissed outright rather than accepted as residual. NO backstop was owed: the finding was `medium`, and the backstop covers only dismissed `high` or `critical`.

THE ONE VALID FINDING WAS DEFECT (12) AGAIN, AND THE TRIAGER OVERRULED THE ORCHESTRATOR'S EARLIER REMEDY. Both round-3 lenses independently found that the PROVENANCE list had NINE bullets for TEN receipts, the missing one being `Q-55-resumecost`. The orchestrator had already been offered this choice once, when the planner raised the stale numeral, and had taken the cheaper half: delete the word "NINE". THAT WAS INSUFFICIENT, and the triager said so plainly: a nine-bullet list under "with decision receipts in `docs/metrics/workflow.jsonl` ... all carrying `task:...`" ASSERTS ITS COUNT IN UNARY, so removing the numeral satisfied the standing rule's letter and missed its subject. IT ALSO REJECTED ADDING THE TENTH BULLET, which is what both reviewers prescribed, on evidence from this very loop: FOUR of the ten receipts landed DURING this review loop and one landed AFTER the round-2 triage had verified the then-current registry, so a tenth bullet restores sync only until the next human decision and round 4 or 5 finds the identical finding with an eleventh. WHAT SURVIVES CONTINUED APPENDS IS THE SELECTOR, NOT A SNAPSHOT: the file path plus the `task:` filter stays correct however many receipts land (One source of truth, which the document invokes by name at two places). Before prescribing deletion it VERIFIED that all ten receipts are separately cited in the body at the section each governs, eight with human-and-date attribution, so the registry carried no information of its own. It also CORRECTED a sub-claim inside the valid finding: "the last taken on 2026-08-02" asserts a DATE, not an identity, and is true today.

THE ROUND 3 FIX PASS IS DONE (commit `dd54227`) AND IT IS THE BEST-CONDITIONED PASS OF THE THREE: A PURE DELETION WITH ZERO AUTHORED WORDS, 1 insertion and 11 deletions per file, the sidecar and the projection identical so nothing is hand-made, ledger and review files untouched, zero line citations. The planner verified BEFORE deleting that each of the ten receipt ids still appears in the document body outside the deleted passage, and reported all ten with line numbers; `Q-55-resumecost` keeps its single body citation at the accepted cost (iv) record, which is the attribution the triage relied on. It also reported, unprompted, that the log GREW UNDER THE FILE while the pass ran, from 255 records to 256, which is defect (12)'s mechanism observed live and is the strongest possible confirmation that deletion rather than a tenth bullet was the right call.

THE TRIAGER'S CONVERGENCE JUDGEMENT, WHICH THE ORCHESTRATOR ACCEPTED AND ACTED ON: DO NOT ESCALATE, RUN ROUNDS 4 AND 5. Its ground: the mechanism, increment split, risk classifications, accepted-cost set and check structure have survived THREE ROUNDS AND SIX INDEPENDENT LENSES with zero falsified claims; every finding across all three rounds was in freshly authored prose; and round 3's single finding was not even that, being a bookkeeping copy going out of sync with a log the orchestrator itself appends to, an artefact of the loop being OPEN rather than a defect in the plan. Two clean rounds is realistic because a zero-authored-word pass leaves nothing for round 4 to catch that round 3 created, and this project now has FOUR independent confirmations that deletion-class passes re-seed nothing. ITS STATED TRIPWIRE, which the orchestrator will honour: ESCALATE IF ROUND 4 RETURNS ANYTHING ABOVE `low`, OR ANY FINDING IN THE MECHANISM RATHER THAN THE PROSE.

ROUND 4 IS OPEN WITH THE LENS THIS LOOP HAS BEEN BUILDING TOWARD. Since the prose seam is mined out and six lenses found ZERO mechanism defects, round 4's primary lens is ADVERSARIAL ATTACK ON THE MECHANISM (opus): its goal is to BREAK inc2 as specified by CONSTRUCTING a false green or an unrecorded false refusal, building fixtures and running them rather than reading, and it is explicitly forbidden from reporting prose, wording, counts or citations. It must keep an ATTACK LOG including the attacks that FAILED, because on a clean result the failed attacks are what makes "no defect found" mean anything. The second lens is DELETION VERIFICATION plus a final whole-document consistency sweep (sonnet). Models swapped again so no lens repeats a model-and-role pairing.

ROUND 4 RAN, THE ADVERSARIAL LENS BROKE THE MECHANISM, AND THE BACKSTOP FIRED FOR THE FIRST TIME IN THIS TASK AND OVERTURNED A DISMISSAL (record 257, outcome `new_valid`, 3 valid: 1 high, 1 medium, 1 low; `dismissal_recheck` record 258 with `result` `overturned`). The verification lens returned ZERO findings, so every round-4 finding came from the adversarial lens. Streak stays 0 of 2 at round 4 of a cap of 5, so EVEN A PERFECTLY CLEAN ROUND 5 REACHES ONLY 1 and CONVERGENCE WITHIN THE CAP IS IMPOSSIBLE; escalation was certain and spending round 5 would have bought nothing.

THE ADVERSARIAL LENS EARNED THE ROUND. Sixteen valid findings across rounds 1 to 3, EVERY ONE IN PROSE, and six lenses had found ZERO mechanism defects; that is precisely why round 4's primary lens was told to BREAK the mechanism by construction rather than read it, and forbidden from reporting prose, counts or citations. IT SUCCEEDED: `R4A-1`, THE CONTAINMENT PREDICATE IS VACUOUS FOR ANY PROJECT NESTED INSIDE THE CHECKED PLAN'S ROOT. A nested project's log lies UNDER that root, so the predicate is silent, `validate --workflow` prints `workflow invariants hold` at exit 0 for a step with no evidence of its own, and `next` prints `state: converged` and `mark the step complete`. CONTAINMENT REFUSES ONLY WHAT LIES OUTSIDE THE SUBTREE. It ran 24 attacks and logged the 21 that FAILED, which is what makes the three that landed mean something; the most dangerous non-break was a `--metrics` escaping the root through a NONEXISTENT intermediate component, which would have defeated the guard by pure prefix match and fails only because the kernel will not open such a path, so the specified resolution is safe there BY AN OS PROPERTY RATHER THAN BY CONSTRUCTION.

THE TRIAGER DISMISSED THE HIGH AND WAS HALF RIGHT, WHICH IS WHY THE BACKSTOP EXISTS. It matched `R4A-1` to round 1's `EX-5`, already ruled valid at `low` and fixed by a deletion that landed, and it CORRECTED THE REVIEWER'S STATED CAUSE by building a fixture with both projects conventionally laid out and NO fallback anywhere, getting the identical false green and proving `Q-55-noconvention`'s fallback is IRRELEVANT to it. Both of those hold and the backstop confirmed both independently. BUT ITS SCOPE SWEEP NAMED THREE SITES AND MISSED THE LEDGER HALF. THE BACKSTOP OVERTURNED THE DISMISSAL on two claims that are ADDED BY THIS FOLD and that the false green falsifies: a WHOLLY INSERTED sentence claiming "The predicate rooted on the checked plan catches that; an anchor-rooted one cannot", against which its fixture had `next` echo a marker line from project A's ledger under the outer project's plan, uncaught; and the `ledger-not-this-project` vocabulary definition, stated as a PROJECT-IDENTITY condition with no containment qualifier, contradicting its own leading clause and matching a second condition the same fold explicitly REJECTS elsewhere. Neither was ruled by any round, verified by grepping all eleven prior review files. The backstop reached its verdict having tried SIX distinct routes to overturn and reporting each that failed, which is the posture it was briefed into: try to overturn, and let the failures make a surviving dismissal safe.

THE BACKSTOP CORRECTED BOTH EARLIER AGENTS ON OWNERSHIP, AND THIS IS THE MOST CONSEQUENTIAL THING IT FOUND. The reviewer assigned the defect to the FALLBACK root cause queued to the validation-constraints step; the triager assigned it to the PROJECT-IDENTITY work queued to the same step. IT BELONGS TO NEITHER: project identity is a filter over `Round` records and `[meta]`, and IT CANNOT CHANGE WHICH LEDGER FILE `run_next` OPENS. So the ledger half of this false green currently has NO OWNER anywhere in the plan. FOUR INDEPENDENT READERS HAVE NOW REDISCOVERED THIS BOUND FROM SCRATCH and the two most recent each got its cause wrong in a DIFFERENT way, which the backstop diagnosed: the document records the bound through a SINGLE INSTANCE, the copied log, whose mechanism is a COPY while the actual rule is a SUBTREE, so readers keep reaching for the wrong cause. Its judgement, labelled as a judgement and separate from its verdict: RECORDING ONE INSTANCE RATHER THAN THE RULE IS NOT ADEQUATE.

ESCALATION DECIDED (2026-08-03, escalation record 259, `human_decision` `decision`): FIX THE FILED FINDINGS, STATE THE RULE, RUN ONE VERIFICATION ROUND, THEN MERGE. Chosen over fixing only what is filed and merging, over changing the mechanism inside inc2, and over authoring a new step for the subtree gap. WHICH BRANCH OF THE COUNTER RULE APPLIES, STATED EXPLICITLY BECAUSE THE TWO BRANCHES GIVE DIFFERENT BARS: this is the "send it back for a specific fix that closes this loop" branch, so THE COUNTERS RETIRE with the decision rather than reset. The RESET branch would be wrong here and would SILENTLY RAISE THE BAR, exactly as step 92's escalation record spells out: this artifact is `risky` at 2 consecutive clean rounds, so a reset plus the one authorised round would end at streak 1 of 2 and converge NOTHING. Under the retire branch the verification round is a CHECK ON THAT SPECIFIC FIX, did it land as prescribed and did it re-seed anything, and a clean result MERGES.

THE `Q-55-endproperty` FOLD IS MERGED (2026-08-03, ff-merged to main at `d11e4ef`). POST-MERGE ALL GREEN: 262 records valid, 95 steps and 69 questions valid, workflow invariants hold, `render --check` up to date, 395 tests passing across eight binaries with 0 failures, clippy silent under `-D warnings`, tree clean, NO worktrees. FINAL SHAPE against main: the sidecar 39 added / 32 removed, the plan TOML 1/1, and the generated projection 40/33, which is exactly the sidecar plus the TOML and therefore carries nothing hand-made.

THE VERIFICATION ROUND WAS NOT CLEAN (record 260, 3 valid: 2 medium, 1 low, plus 1 accepted residual, nothing dismissed, no backstop owed), so the escalation's authorised path of fix-one-round-merge ran out and the artifact went back to the human. `Q-55-verifyclose` DECIDED: APPLY ALL THREE AND MERGE WITH NO FURTHER ROUND, over applying then running one more round, and over fixing only the two mediums. The final pass is `d11e4ef`: 19 authored against 35 deleted, NET MINUS 16, three lines per file, two of the three fixes pure or near-pure deletion, and the one that authors most LIFTS 7 OF ITS 10 WORDS from the vocabulary entry both round-5 lenses had ruled true.

A ZERO-FINDING LENS IS NOT EVIDENCE OF CORRECTNESS UNLESS IT APPLIED THE EVIDENCE IT GATHERED, AND THIS LOOP PROVED IT. The two verification-round lenses reached OPPOSITE conclusions about the same sentence. The truth lens BUILT the reversed-nesting fixture, MEASURED it correctly, and even WROTE that reversing which project contains which flips the verdict, then applied that fixture only to a different claim and tested the sentence on sibling and forward-nested pairs only. IT NEVER CROSSED ITS OWN FALSIFIER WITH THE SENTENCE, and returned zero findings. The triager built both directions itself and ruled for the verification lens. Carry this: a clean round is worth what its lenses actually exercised, not what they collected.

THE MEASURED FIX-PASS RULE IS NOW SHARPER, AND THE OLD STATEMENT OF IT WAS WRONG IN A WAY THAT MATTERED. This ledger has said for several loops that AUTHORED PROSE re-seeds while DELETION does not. The round-5 triager measured the escalation fix and refined it: TWO of the four unpriced sites were CLEAN and ruled TRUE by both lenses, while the three that failed shared ONE property, EACH STATED A SCOPE OR OUTCOME MORE GENERALLY THAN WHAT WAS MEASURED. The symmetric nesting qualifier generalised a DIRECTIONAL behaviour; the fallback-direction clause generalised the backstop's claim about costs (iii) and (iv) into a claim about the fallback while dropping that backstop's own "sufficient and not necessary" result; the ledger-echo clause stated on both surfaces what is true of one. SO THE OPERATIONAL RULE IS NOT "AUTHOR FEWER WORDS", IT IS: STATE CLAIMS IN THE DIRECTION THEY WERE MEASURED, IN VOCABULARY THE DOCUMENT ALREADY DEFINES. Word count is a proxy for that, and a poor one; the winning fix here authored 19 words and was safe because 7 of them were lifted rather than coined.

TWO RESIDUALS ARE CARRIED PAST THE MERGE. DO NOT RE-RAISE EITHER WITHOUT NEW EVIDENCE, AND DO NOT LET THE SECOND VANISH. (1) `R5A-4` (low, ACCEPTED): the escalation fix's prescribed edit at accepted cost (ii) orphaned a second antecedent, "and the guard wins", where the planner repaired only the first; accepted because the clause is NOT false and the same sentence supplies the contrast as a measurement. (2) THE TWO-SURFACE LEDGER DIVERGENCE, `Q-55-ledgerdivergence` DECIDED: RECORD IT AS A NAMED RESIDUAL NOW, over folding it into inc2's own work review and over giving it a backlog step. THE ITEM: the `status --resume` rule is a SAME-ROOT AGREEMENT test between the two named plans, while the `next` ledger rule is a CONTAINMENT test on the checked plan's root, and the sidecar separately claims the predicate is NEVER RE-IMPLEMENTED PER SURFACE. Those are two different tests on one concept. THE ROUND-4 BACKSTOP raised it as adjacent, the round-5 verification lens flagged that it SURVIVES the fix, and NO ROUND HAS RULED ON IT. It is recorded here rather than in the plan because it is a question about a mechanism not yet built; WHOEVER REVIEWS INC2's IMPLEMENTATION MUST RULE ON IT, and it is the first thing to test once both surfaces exist in code and the divergence is measurable rather than argued.

SIX-ROUND SCORECARD FOR CALIBRATION: 22 valid findings across five review rounds plus a verification round (r1 6, r2 9, r3 1, r4 3, verification 3), severities falling 3-high / 2-high / none / 1-high / none, ONE dismissal, ONE BACKSTOP FIRING WHICH OVERTURNED THAT DISMISSAL, and one escalation. THE BACKSTOP'S FIRST FIRING IN THIS TASK'S HISTORY EARNED ITS PLACE IMMEDIATELY: it overturned a well-argued dismissal by finding two claims ADDED BY THE FOLD that the triager's scope sweep had missed, having tried SIX distinct routes to overturn and reported each failure. THE ADVERSARIAL LENS WAS THE OTHER DECISIVE CHOICE: sixteen valid findings across rounds 1 to 3 were ALL PROSE and six lenses found ZERO mechanism defects, and only the lens explicitly told to BREAK the thing rather than read it found the in-root false green. LESSON FOR FUTURE LOOPS, alongside the existing run-executability-early one: WHEN SEVERAL ROUNDS RETURN ONLY PROSE FINDINGS, THAT IS EVIDENCE THE PROSE SEAM IS MINED OUT, NOT EVIDENCE THE ARTIFACT IS SOUND; SWITCH A LENS TO ADVERSARIAL CONSTRUCTION RATHER THAN RUNNING A SEVENTH READER.

THE RECEIPT'S OWN PRECEDENT, checked rather than assumed: five `Q-55-<name>` sub-decision receipts already exist on this step (`Q-55-scope`, `Q-55-mechanism`, `Q-55-noconvention`, `Q-55-refusalscope`, `Q-55-jsonreason`), so a mechanism sub-decision under an already-decided question gets a receipt here even though no new `[[question]]` is registered and W4 requires none. NEW EVIDENCE FOR `Q-69`, recorded where the question can find it: WRITING A DECISION RECEIPT IS NOT IN THE `ISOLATION_POLICY_FRAGMENT`'S ENUMERATION EITHER. The fragment (`src/isolation_policy.rs:ISOLATION_POLICY_FRAGMENT`, rendered to `AGENTS.md:91`) says "The only edits made directly on main are ... : flipping a step's status, declaring an increment, recording a round record, and moving the ledger's resume anchor", four items, and a `type:"decision"` record is none of them. It plainly satisfies the enumeration's OWN governing criterion, being an integration-level record that authors no reviewed product content, exactly like the round record beside it, and this project's history has the orchestrator writing them on main throughout. So this is a SIXTH candidate item for the same exhaustiveness problem `Q-69` already carries from `DEC-1`, and it is the second instance found by simply doing the work rather than by reading the sentence. The receipt was written directly on main on that reasoning; spawning an isolated writer to append one JSONL line would be disproportionate (Minimal by default), and the alternative of not recording the decision at all would lose the auditable evidence the human-input contract was met.

FROZEN HISTORY, SUPERSEDED: the following paragraph described the PLAN review's round 2 fix pass, which was done long ago; the plan review then ran rounds 3 and 4, escalated, took an authorised fix plus one verification round which came back CLEAN from both lenses, and MERGED. Keep it for the fix-pass constraint it states, which still holds, and ignore its "immediate next action".

THE IMMEDIATE NEXT ACTION IS THE ROUND 2 FIX PASS, dispatched to the PLANNER (the producer) in the existing `.claude/worktrees/plan-q55` worktree, covering the TEN valid round-2 findings; the accepted residual `INC2-7` and round 1's accepted residual `F-5` are NOT to be fixed. THE FIX-PASS CONSTRAINT IS LOAD-BEARING AND HAS NOW BEEN VALIDATED PROSPECTIVELY, SO KEEP IT: prefer deletion and narrowing over authored prose, take the MINIMAL fix the triager identified, fix nothing adjacent, and give the planner an explicit report-do-not-fix escape valve whose use is a success rather than a failure. The triager measured that SEVEN OF THE TEN admit a deletion-only or non-prose fix and that only `INC2-5`, `INC2-9` and `INC2-10` need one new clause each. THE ROUND 2 FIX PASS IS DONE (2026-08-01), commit `5169ea0` on `plan/q55-enforcement`, so the IMMEDIATE NEXT ACTION recorded above is DISCHARGED and the next action is round 3. The branch was REBASED onto main `ddfe912` first and the rebase was CLEAN with no JSONL conflict, so the recorded keep-both-sides rule did not fire this time; the rebase also brought the round-2 findings and triage into the worktree as TRACKED files, which satisfies orchestrator defect (2)'s cure (reviewer line numbers must resolve against the reviewed text) without copying anything by hand. All TEN findings were applied and the accepted residuals `INC2-7` and `F-5` were left alone. MEASURED SHAPE, verified by the orchestrator on the branch rather than taken from the planner's report: three files, the artifact at 15 added and 14 removed for net +1 line, the plan TOML at 1/1, and the generated `agent-scaffold.md` at 16/15, which is exactly the artifact's 15/14 plus the TOML's 1/1 and therefore carries nothing hand-made. THE ESCAPE VALVE WAS NOT USED, AND THE DEVIATION RAN THE OTHER WAY, which is the more useful result of this pass. THE TRIAGER'S PRESCRIPTION FOR `INC2-6` WAS ITSELF UNDER-SCOPED and the planner caught it instead of applying it literally. Confirmed first-hand by the orchestrator at `docs/plans/agent-scaffold.steps/workflow-enforcement-tier.md:198-203`: line 198's numeral HEADS the bullet list at 200-203, so the prescribed "Three doc claims" to "Four doc claims" edit WITHOUT a fourth bullet would have left the count contradicting its own list, and the triager's own twin grep (`'Three doc claims'`, count 1) never reached the numeral twin at 354 ("THE THREE DOC COMMENTS ... all three"). The planner took FOUR sites rather than two, and its one authored bullet at 203 is the necessary member. THE LESSON, which belongs in future fix-pass briefs beside the escape valve: A TRIAGER'S SITE COUNT IS A MEASUREMENT, NOT AN INSTRUCTION, and a producer who finds it short should report and widen rather than apply a prescription that manufactures a contradiction. The escape valve covers the case where the prescribed fix is too big or wrong; this is its mirror, where the prescribed fix is too small, and both are successes.

THREE THINGS THE PLANNER NOTICED AND DELIBERATELY DID NOT FIX under the fix-nothing-adjacent constraint, recorded here so round 3 inherits them rather than losing them. (i) Check 11's before-state is now stated against a fixture whose single step is `not-started`, where W3 passes VACUOUSLY, so that check's red is weaker than 14b's. (ii) Line 205's `A FIFTH ITEM` paragraph and the new bullet at 203 describe enumerations short by one in ADJACENT paragraphs, which a fresh reviewer may reasonably read as overlapping. (iii) The metrics log has grown from 240 records to 241, so the sidecar's "386 expected" test count and any record-count claim inside either round-2 review file must be RE-MEASURED by round 3 rather than trusted from the file that states it.

ORCHESTRATOR DEFECT (10), A NEAR-REPEAT OF (8), CAUGHT WITHIN ONE CALL BOTH TIMES. Twice this session the orchestrator wrote a `cd` into a worktree at the head of a command whose real work used `git -C` or needed no working directory at all, and both times that `cd` PERSISTED into the following call. Nothing landed on the wrong branch, because both were caught immediately and the shell was returned to the repo root before anything else ran, but this is the identical mechanism that once put commit `7400f12` and a decision receipt on the branch instead of main and made a `render --check` the orchestrator REPORTED TO THE HUMAN vacuous. THE RECORDED CURE, "never let a `cd` into a worktree persist", IS TOO WEAK, because it asks the orchestrator to remember to undo something after the fact. THE STRENGTHENED CURE, which replaces it: never type the `cd` at all. Use `git -C <path>`, absolute paths, or a subshell `(cd <path> && ...)` whose scope ends with the command. A bare `cd` into a worktree is a defect ON SIGHT, whether or not a correction follows it.

TWO CHANGES TO THE ROUND 3 BRIEFS, BOTH EARNED BY MEASURED FAILURES AND NEITHER OPTIONAL. (1) THE TENSE RULE, which is the most transferable lesson this review has produced: BOTH demonstrated coverage misses in this review happened because a reviewer tested a claim about a FUTURE state the way you test a claim about the CURRENT tree, opened the cited code, confirmed the sentence is true of the tree, and passed it. A PLAN IS A SET OF CLAIMS ABOUT WHAT WILL EXIST, so that habit systematically passes exactly the sentences a plan exists to get right. Brief every plan-review lens to evaluate each claim AGAINST WHAT THE INCREMENT PRODUCES, not against the tree, and to say which tense it applied when the two differ. (2) ASSIGN FINDING-ID PREFIXES UP FRONT, per reviewer, because both round-2 reviewers independently used `R2-1` for different findings and the triager had to rename them; that is orchestrator defect (9), assigning distinct FILENAMES but not distinct ID NAMESPACES. ALSO KEEP, having earned their place: the enumeration discipline (reviewers LIST what they swept rather than asserting thoroughness, which is what made the round-2 miss visible and attributable), the explicit permission that a clean result is a good outcome and findings must not be manufactured, and reviewer MODEL DIVERSITY.

ROUND 3 RAN AND IS TRIAGED (2026-08-01, record 240, outcome `new_valid`, streak still 0 of 2). Two worktree-isolated lenses, both created FROM THE BRANCH TIP `5169ea0` so citations resolved against the reviewed text: a residue and fix-verification lens (`claude-sonnet-5`, 1 finding) and a FRESH DEEP READ of inc1, inc3, the risk-classification arguments and the increment ordering (`claude-opus-5`, 7 findings). 8 raw, 8 after dedup, 8 VALID, 0 accept-residual, 0 dismissed, ALL EIGHT `low`. No high or critical raised or dismissed, so no backstop re-check was owed. The nine review files now on main are the six listed above plus `-r3-reviewer-residue.md`, `-r3-reviewer-inc13.md` and `-r3-triage.md`.

THE SEVERITY TREND IS MONOTONE AND IS THE STRONGEST CONVERGENCE SIGNAL THIS FOLD HAS PRODUCED: round 1 was 15 findings with 1 high and 4 medium, round 2 was 11 with 0 high and 3 medium, round 3 is 8 with 0 high and ZERO MEDIUM. Counts falling 15 to 11 to 8, top severity falling high to medium to low. ZERO findings landed in inc2, the increment that took ten last round and had just been fixed, which is direct evidence the round-2 fix pass closed its material rather than moving it.

THE DELIBERATE COVERAGE CORRECTION WAS RIGHT AND SHOULD BE REPEATED. Inc1 and inc3 had never had a dedicated deep read (round 1 read the whole sidecar as instructions and as fidelity; round 2's deep lens read inc2 only), and the fresh lens produced 7 of the 8 findings. FOUR of its seven are the same shape: A DEFECT AN EARLIER ROUND ESTABLISHED, SURVIVING AT A TWIN SITE THE EARLIER FIX'S GREP COULD NOT MATCH. `R3B-1` is the clearest and is the most important finding of the round: the ordering argument's premise at `:290`, that inc3 is "the only increment that makes a previously-green run fail", is falsified TWICE INSIDE THE SAME DOCUMENT, by inc2's own risk paragraph at `:298` and by inc1's acceptance check, and it is round 1's `EX-5` at a site the `EX-5` fix could not reach. That is now the third time this fold has been bitten by a twin a literal string grep cannot match, which is the standing argument for the semantic-twin sweep added to the triage brief.

THE TENSE RULE PAID FOR ITSELF ON ITS FIRST OUTING, which settles whether to keep it. `R3B-3`: check 1 pins `cargo test` at "386 expected", THE PRE-CHANGE COUNT, while `:304` requires each increment to add tests, so the check AS WRITTEN PASSES an implementation that adds no test and FAILS a correct one. That sentence is TRUE OF TODAY'S TREE and FALSE OF THE TREE THE INCREMENT PRODUCES, which is precisely the class the rule was written to catch and precisely the class three prior rounds walked past while confirming the count was current. `R3B-5` is the same rule applied to check 15, whose "reports the missing log BY PATH" is already true of the pre-fix build, so only the exit code discriminates and the literal minimal implementation ships a problem reading "skipping the workflow check" on a run that exits 1 while passing every check. THE ID-PREFIX FIX ALSO WORKED: `R3A-` and `R3B-` collided nowhere and the triager renamed nothing, against round 2 where both reviewers used `R2-1`. Keep assigning prefixes per reviewer.

THE TRIAGER DECLINED BOTH AVAILABLE SHORTCUTS, IN OPPOSITE DIRECTIONS. It ruled all eight VALID with zero accept-residual and zero dismissals, AND it considered `medium` for `R3B-1` and `R3B-5` and HELD `low` on both, recording that severity is absolute impact if unfixed and that lineage from an earlier `medium` "is no more a merit than the round a finding arrives in". That is the step-92 round-4 triager's formula applied in the OPPOSITE direction, against inflation rather than against dismissal, and it is the second time this project's triage layer has stated the round-number-is-not-a-merit rule unprompted. It also RULED AGAINST MERGING `R3A-1` with `R3B-5` despite their shared theme (an acceptance check clause satisfied on both sides of the change, round 1's `EX-8` genus), distinguishing them by which half of the check is weak, by what the fix edits, and by root cause.

CITATIONS CORRECTED AND FACTS ADDED BY THE TRIAGER, the `Q-66` grounding rule earning its keep for the fourth consecutive loop. `R3A-1`'s `src/workflow.rs:437` does NOT support the claim attached to it, and NO code citation could, since the predicate in question is inc2's UNBUILT work; the finding survives with that citation struck and its true grounds at `:164` and `:180`. The triager further recorded that `R3B-1`'s OWN PROPOSED FALLBACK WORDING is false of inc2's refusal, so the deletion is the only correct form, which is a triager pre-empting a fix pass from authoring the very defect it is closing. TWO FACTS NEITHER REVIEWER HAD, each changing an outcome: explorer A's false pass 2 was measured on a BORROWED-SLUG fixture, which settles `R3A-1` against dismissal; and `.agents/LEDGER.template.md` and `.agents/prompts/orchestrator.md` ARE deployed without `--instrument`, which removes the "the missed sites do not ship" defence against `R3B-4`.

THE ROUND 3 FIX PASS IS THE BEST-CONDITIONED OF THE THREE, on the measure this project has calibration data for. NINE sites total: FIVE DELETIONS across four findings, one narrowing, two clauses or substitutions inside existing checks, and only ONE (`R3B-2`, the missing `CHANGELOG.md` entry in the inc3 documentation-impact list) authoring new text. Eight sites are in the primary sidecar and one is in `test-tmpdir-repo-assumption.md:66`. Given the three independent confirmations that deletion-class passes re-seed nothing while prose-authoring passes manufacture the next round's finding, this is the pass most likely to produce a clean round 4.

ARITHMETIC AFTER ROUND 3, stated plainly because it now constrains everything: STREAK 0 OF 2, with rounds 4 and 5 left against a cap of 5, so CONVERGENCE INSIDE THE CAP REQUIRES BOTH REMAINING ROUNDS TO COME BACK CLEAN. A single valid finding in round 4 caps the reachable streak at 1 and makes escalation certain, which is the position step 92 reached at its round 4. Convergence is still reachable here and was not there.

THE ROUND 3 FIX PASS IS DONE (2026-08-01), commit `1a1655c` on `plan/q55-enforcement`, all EIGHT findings closed across NINE sites with exactly ONE authored line (`R3B-2`'s `CHANGELOG.md` bullet). Verified by the orchestrator on the branch rather than taken from the report: two source files at 8 added / 7 removed and 1 / 1, ASCII counts 0 on every changed file, validators green, and the generated `agent-scaffold.md` REGENERATED by `render` rather than hand-edited. NEITHER the escape valve NOR the widening was used, and the planner ARGUED WHY the widening trigger was not met rather than simply not reaching for it: the `INC2-6` trigger is "applying the prescription literally manufactures a contradiction", and deleting an expectation leaves no list or count out of step with anything. That is the mirror rule applied with judgement instead of mechanically, which is what it is for. The planner also CORRECTED A TRIAGE MEASUREMENT, not load-bearing: the triage records `skipping the workflow check` as having zero sites in the fold, where it has one, at `:47`, inside the verbatim pre-fix reproduction transcript; that site is correctly framed as today's stderr and stays true, so the triager's conclusion holds and only the count was wrong.

TWO ITEMS WERE DISCLOSED BY THE PRODUCER AND THE HUMAN DECIDED TO CLOSE BOTH BEFORE ROUND 4 (2026-08-01), over the two alternatives of closing only the fix-induced one and of running round 4 unchanged with both disclosed to its triager. (i) FIX-INDUCED, and the only one this fold has produced that the producer caught itself: `R3B-1`'s prescribed deletion struck the clause that was the antecedent for "when it does" at `:290`, leaving a dangling referent, and the planner LEFT IT DELIBERATELY because the only repair it could see was the replacement clause the triager had forbidden, then disclosed it. (ii) TRIAGE-PRE-BLESSED: `R3B-3` was scoped to the two sites pinning `cargo test` at 386, and the triage recorded of the three remaining NARRATIVE sites that they are not framed as historical so "they will be wrong of the post-increment tree", that "the same deletion works at all three", and that "the fix pass may take all five in one deletion pass and I would not call that over-scope".

THE ORCHESTRATOR PUT THAT TO THE HUMAN RATHER THAN DECIDING IT, and the reason is worth keeping for the next loop: closing those items means PRE-EMPTING REVIEWERS on a prediction of what round 4 would raise, which is a role boundary rather than a routine integration call. THE COST WAS STATED AND ACCEPTED: a clean round 4 is now LESS INFORMATIVE, because two likely findings were removed by prediction rather than by review, and any future reading of this loop's convergence must discount round 4 accordingly. WHAT MADE IT DEFENSIBLE, recorded so the precedent is bounded rather than general: both items are PURE DELETIONS with zero authored words, which is the one fix class this project has measured as re-seeding nothing; item (ii) TAKES AN OPTION THE TRIAGE LEFT OPEN IN WRITING rather than overruling a scoping decision; and item (i) is the PRODUCER FINISHING ITS OWN PASS rather than a third party second-guessing a ruling. The supplementary pass was dispatched to the PLANNER, never authored by the orchestrator, with ZERO AUTHORED WORDS as a hard constraint and an explicit escape valve for any site that cannot be closed by deletion alone. IF A FUTURE LOOP FACES THIS AGAIN AND EITHER CONDITION IS ABSENT, in particular if the fix would author prose or would overrule rather than extend a triage ruling, the answer should be to run the round instead.

THE SUPPLEMENTARY PASS LANDED AT `e34c2c9` and closed both items, verified on the branch: four files at one to three lines each, `386` returning NOTHING anywhere in the artifact set afterwards, and zero new vocabulary. The one edit that is not literally a deletion substitutes `tests` for `of 386` in a plan TOML step title, and `tests` is already the third word of that same title string, so nothing new enters the document. THE PASS THEN DISCLOSED A THIRD ITEM, which is the one that decided round 4: `workflow-enforcement-tier.md:280` claims inc2 "is the only part of the mechanism that changes what a currently-succeeding invocation REPORTS", which is the SAME EXCLUSIVITY SHAPE round 1 struck at `:272` (`EX-5`) and round 3 struck at `:290` (`R3B-1`). THE ORCHESTRATOR DID NOT CLOSE IT, applying the bound it had just recorded: all three conditions were absent (it needs a substitution rather than a deletion, no triager had ruled on it, and it is pre-existing rather than created by the pass), and the recorded answer for that case is to run the round. Writing a rule and then stepping around it the first time it costs something would have made the rule worthless.

ROUND 4 RAN AND IS TRIAGED (2026-08-01, record 241, outcome `new_valid`, streak still 0 of 2). Two lenses from the branch tip `e34c2c9`: a residue lens over BOTH recent passes (`claude-sonnet-5`, 1 finding) and a dedicated INTERNAL CONSISTENCY lens (`claude-opus-5`, 7 findings) briefed to read the document AGAINST ITSELF rather than against the code. 8 raw, 7 after dedup, 7 VALID, 0 accept-residual, 0 dismissed, ALL `low`. The triager considered `medium` for the merged `:280` finding and for `R4B-4` and held `low` on both.

THE CONSISTENCY LENS WAS CHOSEN FROM EVIDENCE AND IT PAID, and the choice is the transferable lesson of this round. By round 3 the single most productive defect class in this artifact was A CLAIM IN THIS DOCUMENT THAT ANOTHER CLAIM IN THIS DOCUMENT FALSIFIES, and each instance had been invisible to the rounds before it because reviewers check claims against THE CODE, which is where a citation points, rather than against the document's OTHER CLAIMS, which is where this document's contradictions live. Pointing a lens at the class rather than at its instances returned SEVEN contradictions that four prior rounds had read past: a stated "five measured grounds" whose own fifth bullet says four, "two responses" against "three responses" across five sites, a ledger default described two ways, `resume_state` causes counted two ways, and a doc-comment count still reading "three" at three sites after round 3 corrected it at two. THE LENS WAS DELIBERATELY NOT TOLD about `:280`, so its independent arrival there is confirmation rather than echo. BRIEF A CONSISTENCY LENS EARLY on any artifact assembled in layers over multiple passes.

THE `:280` LINEAGE IS SETTLED AND IT IS A MISSED TWIN, NOT FIX-INDUCED RESIDUE. The triager verified with `git log -S` that the line traces to `8db3f83`, which PRECEDES `47c6460`, `d9726fa`, `1a1655c` and `e34c2c9`, so all three sites of the exclusivity family were authored together and both earlier greps were faithfully scoped and simply could not match this wording. THE DISTINCTION MATTERS AND SHOULD NOT BE COLLAPSED: fix-induced residue would mean the fix passes are manufacturing defects, while a missed twin means the SWEEP is the thing failing, and only the second is true here. That keeps the deletion-class-passes-re-seed-nothing finding intact, since no fix pass produced this.

THE TRIAGER REJECTED THREE OF THE REVIEWERS' PRESCRIBED FIXES AND PREFERRED ONE REVIEWER'S ANALYSIS OVER THE OTHER'S, which is the adjudication layer doing real work rather than collating. It merged `R4A-1` into `R4B-3` and ruled `R4B-3` CORRECT: `R4A-1` conceded the "failing" disjunct to inc1 but claimed "withholding" is exclusive to inc2, and that is wrong, because checks 3, 5, 6 and 7 require inc1 ALONE to withhold on the validator and on all three projections, so BOTH halves of the parenthetical are false of inc1. It REJECTED `R4B-2`'s numeral edit because "five measured grounds" immediately heads a five-bullet list, so changing it to "four" would recreate the round 2 trap exactly, and prescribed deleting the numeral instead. It REJECTED `R4B-6`'s substitutions because they would leave two sites both saying "three consumers" with DIFFERENT memberships, moving the defect rather than closing it. It TESTED and rejected a smaller edit for `R4B-7` because it would put a "two" directly above a three-bullet list. It also CORRECTED `R4B-1`'s round attribution (`INC2-6` is a round 2 finding fixed in `d9726fa`; round 3 only verified it) and established the true doc-comment count INDEPENDENTLY as four, finding that "three" matches no reading of the evidence. `R4B-5`'s reachability it confirmed BY RUNNING THE TOOL rather than reading the code.

THE ROUND 4 FIX SHAPE: 11 edit points across 10 lines, being 5 deletions, 4 numeral or numeral-qualifier edits, 1 narrowing, 1 clause appended inside an existing sentence, ZERO new sentences and ZERO new bullets. Nine of the eleven author no words at all; the two that do author 14 words total, all of it existing in-document vocabulary. One line, `:298`, carries two independent fixes. A re-render is owed, with 10 mirror sites in the generated view.

ESCALATION IS NOW CERTAIN AND IS OWED TO THE HUMAN. The streak is 0 of 2 and round 5 is the cap, so the maximum reachable streak is 1 against a `risky` bar of 2 and CONVERGENCE INSIDE THE CAP IS ARITHMETICALLY IMPOSSIBLE regardless of round 5's outcome. This is the same position step 92 reached at its own round 4, and the same rule applies: when the human's decision is applied the counters either RESET or RETIRE depending on which branch is chosen, so the question is not "fix or ship" but how to spend the escalation. THE TREND THE HUMAN NEEDS ALONGSIDE THE ARITHMETIC: valid findings 14, 10, 8, 7 across the four rounds, with top severity falling high, medium, low, low, and rounds 3 and 4 producing ZERO findings of medium or above. Every round 4 finding is an editorial contradiction about counts and scope wording; NONE touches the mechanism, the increment division, the ordering argument or the risk classifications, all of which have now survived four rounds including two dedicated deep reads.

ESCALATION DECIDED (2026-08-01, escalation record 242, `human_decision` `decision`): APPLY THE SEVEN FIXES, RUN ONE VERIFICATION ROUND, THEN MERGE ON A CLEAN RESULT. Chosen over merging with no further round, over resetting for a fresh two-clean-round loop, and over accepting all seven as residuals. WHICH BRANCH OF THE COUNTER RULE APPLIES, stated explicitly because the two branches give different bars and step 92 showed the wrong pick silently raises them: this is the "send it back for a specific fix that closes this loop" branch, so THE COUNTERS RETIRE WITH THE DECISION rather than reset. Under the reset branch the artifact would be `risky` at streak 0 again, and the one authorised round would end at 1 of 2 and converge nothing. Under the retire branch the verification round is A CHECK ON THAT SPECIFIC FIX (did the eleven edit points land as prescribed, did any re-seed), not the first round of a fresh convergence loop, and a clean result MERGES.

THE FIX AS AUTHORISED, so the planner has no latitude to widen it: the seven triaged findings, at the eleven edit points the round 4 triage prescribes, being 5 deletions, 4 numeral or numeral-qualifier edits, 1 narrowing, and 1 clause appended inside an existing sentence, with ZERO new sentences and ZERO new bullets. Eight of the eleven author no words; the three that do insert 17 words gross, all existing in-document vocabulary (this sentence originally read "nine" and "two ... 14 words", copied from the triage's round-summary; the verification round's mechanical lens re-counted and found THREE inserting edit points, and the triage's own per-finding text for `R4B-7` already said "Three words inserted", so the summary was the thing that slipped, not the fixes). The triager's REJECTIONS are part of the authorisation and must be honoured rather than re-derived: `R4B-2` takes a numeral DELETION and not the reviewer's "four", because "five measured grounds" heads a five-bullet list and the substitution would recreate the round 2 trap; `R4B-6` takes three PURE DELETIONS and not the reviewer's "consumers" substitutions, which would leave two sites both saying "three consumers" with different memberships and move the defect rather than close it; `R4B-7` takes the triager's form, a smaller edit having been tested and rejected because it would put a "two" directly above a three-bullet list. `:298` carries TWO independent fixes. A RE-RENDER IS OWED, with 10 mirror sites in the generated view.

THE AUTHORISED FIX LANDED AT `61fc8b2` AND THE VERIFICATION ROUND WAS CLEAN FROM BOTH LENSES (2026-08-01, record 243, outcome `clean`, `consecutive_clean` 1). The fix is 3 files, 20 insertions and 20 deletions, eleven edit points on ten lines, and all eleven landed in the prescribed form. THE THREE PRESCRIPTIONS WHERE THE TRIAGER OVERRODE A REVIEWER ALL LANDED IN THE TRIAGER'S FORM, which the verification round checked specifically: `R4B-2` as a pure numeral deletion rather than the "four" substitution that would have recreated the round 2 trap, `R4B-6` as three pure deletions rather than the "consumers" substitutions that would have moved the defect, and the merged `R4A-1`/`R4B-3` as the triager's fuller deletion rather than the shorter one. `:298`'s two independent fixes both landed with neither overwriting the other. LENS B (consistency, the round 4 winner re-pointed at the fix's own riskiest property) RE-DERIVED 28 COUNTS FROM SCRATCH rather than checking the ones the fix wrote, and all 28 agree. IT EXAMINED FOUR CANDIDATES AND DECLINED TO RAISE ALL FOUR WITH WRITTEN REASONING, and on the closest call its ground was that a repair would author replacement prose in the document whose own calibration data says prose-authoring fixes manufacture the next round's finding. THAT IS THIS PROJECT'S CALIBRATION DATA PAYING BACK INTO REVIEWER JUDGEMENT, which is what it was collected for.

BOTH LENSES UPHELD THE PRODUCER'S DECISION NOT TO FIX `:170`. Correctly out of scope.

ONE BOOKKEEPING CORRECTION THE VERIFICATION ROUND PRODUCED, recorded because the orchestrator propagated the error to the human: the "nine of eleven author no words, the two that do author 14 words" figure is WRONG and is corrected above. Three edit points insert words, 17 gross. The error originated in the round 4 triage's ROUND-SUMMARY while that same file's per-finding text for `R4B-7` said "Three words inserted", so the aggregate contradicted its own detail. THE LESSON IS THE ONE THIS FOLD KEEPS TEACHING: an aggregate restating a detail elsewhere in the same document is exactly where this artifact's defects live, and that is as true of the REVIEW files as of the artifact they review. Zero new sentences and zero new bullets both verified true, and every individual edit matched its own per-finding prescription, so nothing about the artifact changed.

THE FOLD IS MERGED AND THE PLAN REVIEW IS CLOSED (2026-08-01). FF-merged to main at `424b2b8`.
